8. kutiraic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvaṉmē loruvaṉ kutiraiyēṟic cilliyeṟintāṭum āṭṭu kutiraic cilli.
āṭuvār tokai : itai āṭa iruvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟattilum, teruvilum piṟa veḷiyiṭaṅ kaḷilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭukaruvi : āḷukkoṉṟāka iru kaṟkaḷ allatu ōṭukaḷē itaṟkut tēvai. avai cilliyeṉappaṭum. 
āṭumuṟai : iruvar oru ciṟu kuḻi killi ataṉiṉṟu aintāṟu kacattolaiviṟ kīṟappaṭṭa uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉtaṉ cilliyai eṟivar. kuḻikkup pakkamāka eṟintavaṉ muntiyāṭuvāṉ.
muntiyāṭukiṟavaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ mutukiṉmēl ēṟikkoḷvāṉ. cumakkiṟavaṉ taṉ cilliyai muṉṉāl caṟṟut tolaiviṟku eṟivāṉ. ēṟiyiruppavaṉ atait taṉ cilliyāl aṭittal vēṇṭum. aṭittuviṭiṉ, mēliruntukoṇṭē aṭutta muṟaiyum aṭikkalām. aṭiyāviṭiṉ, kīḻē iṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭai muṭiyum. ovvōr āṭṭaikkum cilli eṟintu, yār muntiyāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.
mēlē yirukkiṟavaṉ, tāṉē yaṭikkāmal, taṉṉaic cumantukoṇṭirukkiṟavaṉaiyum avaṉ eṟinta cilliyai          aṭikkac collām, aṉṟu nī aṭikkiṉṟāyā? nāṉē aṭikkaṭṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. cumakkiṟavaṉ nīyē aṭi eṉṟāl, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ aṭippāṉ; aṉṟi, nāṉ           aṭikkiṟēṉ eṉṟāl, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ cumakkiṟavaṉiṭam taṉ cilliyaik koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. cumakkiṟavaṉ piṉpu aṭippāṉ. aṭipaṭṭuviṭiṉ, mēlirukkiṟavaṉ kīḻē yiṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum; paṭāviṭiṉ, tāṉ kelikkumvarai allatu mēlirukkiṟavaṉ tōṟkumvarai, cumakkiṟavaṉ cumantu koṇṭē yiruttal vēṇṭum. mēlirukkiṟavaṉ kīḻiṟaṅka āṭṭai muṭiyum.
2. peṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. taṭṭāṅkal
perumpāṉmai iruvarum ciṟupāṉmai palarumāṉa makaḷir ciṟu kaṟkaḷaik kaiyāl taṭṭippiṭikkum viḷaiyāṭṭu, taṭṭāṅkal. itu paṇṭaik kālattil kaḻaṅku koṇṭu āṭappaṭṭatiṉāl, kaḻaṅku eṉa vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaḻaṅkāvatu kaḻaṟkāy allatu kaḻaṟcikkāy. kaḻaṟcikkāy eṉpatu iṉṟu keccakkāy eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ceṟiyaric cilampiṟ kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
 polañcey kaḻaṅkiṟ ṟeṟṟi yāṭum	(puṟam. 36)
 makaḷir.... muttavārmaṇaṟ
  poṟkaḻaṅ kāṭum.	(perumpāṇ. 327 - 35)
taṭṭāṅkal kīḻvarumāṟu palavakaippaṭum. avaiyaṉaittum vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭumuṟṟattilum viḷaiyāṭap peṟum.
I. mūṉṟāṅkal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷaik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu mūṉṟāṅkal.
āṭumuṟai : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟaik kīḻvaittu ēṉaiyiraṇṭaiyum kaiyil vaittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ           oṉṟai mēleṟintu iṉṉoṉṟaik kīḻvaittu muntik kīḻvaittatai eṭuttukkoṇṭu, mēleṟintataip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mīṇṭum oṉṟai mēleṟintu, kaiyiluḷ        ḷataik kīḻ vaittuk kīḻiruntatai eṭuttuk koṇṭu mēleṟintataip piṭittal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟē paṉṉirumuṟai toṭarntu tavaṟātu āṭiviṭiṉ, paḻamām. paṉṉirumuṟaikkum kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum.
  (1)	oṉṟāvatu oṉṟāṅkāy.
  (2)	iraṇṭāvatu irattiṉakkiḷi (allatu īccaṅkāy.)
  (3)	mūṉṟāvatu muttuccaram.
  (4)	nālāvatu nāṟkāli.
  (5)	añcāvatu pañcavarṇam.
  (6)	āṟāvatu pālāṟu.
  (7)	ēḻāvatu eḻuttāṇi.
  (8)	eṭṭāvatu koṭṭāram.
  (9)	oṉpatāvatu ōlaippū.
(10)	pattāvatu paṉaṅkoṭṭai.
(11)	patiṉoṉṟāvatu teṉṉam piḷḷai.
(12)	teṉṉaimarat taṭiyilē tērōṭum piḷḷaiyār.
orutti āṭumpōtu tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaḷ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭiṉavaḷ maṟumuṟaiyāṭumpōtu, mīṇṭum mutaliliruntē āṭal vēṇṭum.
II. aintāṅkal (oruvakai)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintu kaṟkaḷaikkoṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu aintāṅkal.
āṭumuṟai : muntiyāṭupavaḷ, inta āṭṭiṟkuriya 
aintu kaṟkaḷaiyum oruṅkē citaṟi, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai 
eṭuttu mēlē pōṭṭuk, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiyeṭuttuk koṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. piṉpu, kaiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟai mēlē pōṭṭuppōṭṭu, ovvoru taṭavaiyum kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟai yeṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭuñ citaṟi oru kallaiyeṭuttu mēleṟintu, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭai eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, kaiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai mēleṟintu, kīḻirukkum ēṉaiyiraṇṭaiyum eṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam aintumuṟai citaṟip piṭikkumpōtu, mūṉṟām muṟai oṉṟum mūṉṟumākavum, nālām muṟai nālaiyum oruṅkēyum, aintām muṟai mūṉṟum oṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kāykaḷai eṭuttalvēṇṭum. im muṟaikaḷ muṟaiyē, oṉṟāṅkoṭṭai, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭai, mūṉṟāṅkoṭṭai, nālāṅkoṭṭai, aintāṅkoṭṭai eṉa avvav viṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
ataṉpiṉ nālu kaṟkaḷaik kaikkuḷ vaittukkoṇṭu, oru kallai iru taṭavai mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittal vēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavai āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttuk I“nfhÊ kokkām eṉṟum, iraṇṭām taṭavai kuttukkaiyāl nilattiṟ kuttik kuttuviḷakkām eṉṟum, mēleṟinta kallaip piṭikku muṉ collavēṇṭum. piṉpum, avvāṟoru kallai iru taṭavai mēleṟintu piṭittalvēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavai piṭikkumuṉ ēṉai nālukaṟkaḷaiyum kīḻē vaittu vaittu eṭuppām eṉṟum, maṟutaṭavai piṭikkumuṉ an nāṉkaiyum vārikkoṇṭu vārikkoṇṭām eṉṟum, collavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, iru kaikaḷaiyum cērttuk kūṭṭukkaiyāka vaittukkoṇṭu, aiṅkallum kīḻē viḻātavāṟu, tappu tāḷam talaivali mōḷam eṉṟu nāṟcīrpaṭac collik koṇṭu, valakkaiyaip puṟaṅkaiyum akaṅkaiyum puṟaṅkaiyum akaṅkaiyumāka irutaṭavai puraṭṭi vaittal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉ, aiṅkallaiyum mēleṟintu piṭittu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaic cokkal vēṇṭum. cokkutalāvatu cilupputal.
piṉpu aiṅkallaiyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkaimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri piṭikkaccoṉṉa kallaip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittukkāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kāṭṭiyapiṉ atait taṉiyāy eṭuttuvaittuviṭṭu, ēṉai nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟai mēleṟintu mūṉṟaik kīḻē vaittuviṭṭup piṭittu, mīṇṭum 
I.  it toṭar cēlattil kōḻippīyām eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
atai mēleṟintu kīḻē vaitta kallai vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum; allatu, valak kuṭaṅkaiyiluḷḷa nāṟkaṟkaḷaiyum ivviraṇṭāka iru taṭavai caṟṟē mēleṟintu, avaṟṟaip puṟammēṉōkkiya iṭakkuṭaṅkaiyāl uṭaṉuṭaṉ piṭittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ paḻamām.
orutti āṭumpōtu, mēleṟintu piṭikkuṅ kal tavaṟiṉālum, kīḻirukkuṅ kallai yeṭukkumpōtu piṟa kallait toṭṭuviṭṭālum, avaḷ niṉṟuviṭavēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaḷ āṭuvāḷ. orutti orē āṭṭaiyil maṟumuṟai allatu vaḻimuṟai āṭumpōtu, muṉpu viṭṭatiliruntu āṭuvāḷ. ivai ellāvakaikkum potuvām.
āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ, veṉṟavaḷ tōṟṟavaḷiṉ kaikaṭ kiṭaiyil oru  kallai vaittu, mēṟkaimēl mūṉṟu taṭavai kuttuvatu vaḻakkam. itu ellā vakaikkum potu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kaiyum kainarampum inta āṭṭāl urampeṟum.  ituvum potuvaram.
aintāṅkal (maṟṟoru vakai)
nālāṅ koṭṭaikkuppiṉ, aiṅkallaiyuṅ kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai muṉṉatiṟpōl nāṉku taṭavai mēlē pōṭṭup pōṭṭu atai ovvoru taṭavaiyum kīḻirukkuṅ kallai ovvoṉṟāy iṭappakkamākac caṟṟut taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, maṟupaṭiyum ellāvaṟṟaiyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai muṉpōṟ palataṭavai mēleṟintu, atai muṟpaṭṭa ovvoru taṭavaiyum kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ nīṅkiyiruppavaṟṟai ovvoṉṟāka neruṅka vaittup piṭittu, iṟutiyil kīḻiruppa vaṟṟaiyellām oruṅkē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ iru pātaṅkaḷaiyum kūṭṭivaittu, avaṟṟiṉ mēl mūlaikkoṉṟāka nāṉmūlaikkum nālu kalvaittu, ēṉaiyoṉṟai nāṉkutaṭavai mēleṟintu, atai ovvoru taṭavaiyum pātaṅkaḷiṉ mēluḷḷa kallai ovvoṉṟāy irupāta iṭaikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṟaku mīṇṭum, aiṅkallaiyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai muṉpōl nāṉku taṭavai mēleṟintu, atai ovvoru taṭavaiyum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷai ovvoṉṟāy iṭappuṟam nilattiṟ pottic caṟṟē tiṟantuvaikkappaṭṭirukkum iṭak kuṭaṅ kaikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, aiṅkallaiyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkaimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri cuṭṭiyataip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittuk kāṭṭi, atait taṉiyē eṭuttuvaittuviṭṭu, ēṉai nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟai mēleṟintu, mūṉṟaik kīḻē vaittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum atai mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟai vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. atōṭu paḻam.
III. ēḻāṅkal (oruvakai)
ēḻāṅkalliṉ ivvakai ēṟattāḻa aintāṅkalliṉ mutalvakai pōṉṟatē.
ēḻu kallaiyum uruṭṭi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiyeṭuttu mēleṟintu oṉṟāṅkoṭṭai mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭai varai yāṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅ koṭṭaiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭaiyil ivviraṇṭākavum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭ ṭaiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅ koṭṭaiyil iraṇṭum nālumākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭaiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅkoṭṭaiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅkoṭ ṭaiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkappeṟum.
piṉpu, muṟaiyē, iḻuttal kuttal vaittal vāral nāṉkum tappu-tāḷam-talaivali-mōḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum.
ataṉpiṉ, ellāk kaṟkaḷaiyum mēleṟintu puṟaṅkaiyāl tāṅkavēṇṭum. mūṉṟu kalmaṭṭum puṟaṅkaimēl niṟpiṉ, avai kaṭṭāṉ karuṅkal eṉak kīḻē pōṭappaṭum. ataṟku mēluṅ kīḻum niṟpiṉ cokka vēṇṭum.
mīṇṭum ellāvaṟṟaiyum muṉpōṟ puṟaṅkaiyil tāṅki, etiri cuṭṭiyataip piṭittukkāṭṭit taṉiyāka vaittuviṭṭu, eñciyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai mēleṟintu aintaik kīḻvaittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum atai mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟai vārip piṭittalvēṇṭum; piṭittuviṭiṉ paḻam.
ēḻāṅkal (maṟṟoru vakai)
oru kallai vaittukkoṇṭu eñciya āṟu kallaiyum uruṭṭi oṉṟāṅkoṭṭai mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭai varai āṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅkoṭṭaiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭaiyil ivviraṇṭākavum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭ ṭaiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅkoṭṭaiyil nālum iraṇṭumākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭaiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅkoṭṭaiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅkoṭ ṭaiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkappaṭum.
kīḻirunteṭukkuṅ kaṟkaḷai orē kaikkuḷ aṭakka iyalātār, irukaiyaiyum payaṉpaṭuttik koḷvatumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, itu attuṇaic ciṟappiṉataṉṟu; aṉaivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatumaṉṟu :
kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ tūrattūra iruntāl, kaikkallaip pakkattil vaittuviṭṭut tolaiviluḷḷa kallai eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
ēḻukoṭṭaikkum piṉvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum:
(1)	poṟukki ciṟukki pō(ki)ṟāḷām taṇṇīrkkut taṇṇīrk kuṭameṭuttu.
(2)	(allatu, vēṟu ētēṉumoṉṟu.) iraṇṭu irumpu, ēḻaṭik karumpu.
(3)	mūṉṟu mukkōṭu, murukaṉ ceṅkōṭu.
(4)	nāṉku naṭalam, tēṅkāyp puṭalam.
(5)	aivar araikkum mañcaḷ tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ.
(6)	ākkūr aṭivāḻai, aṇṇaṉ tampi peruvāḻai.
(7)	ēḻaṇṇaṉ kāṭṭilē, eṅkaḷaṇṇaṉ rōṭṭilē, mañcaḷ cāraṭṭilē.
iṉṉoru pāṭṭup piṉvarumāṟu :
(1)	tūppoṟukki tūtuḷaṅkāy
	 māppoṟukki mātuḷaṅkāy
	 kal poṟukki kaṭāraṅkāy.
(2)	īr īrttikkoḷ
	 pūppaṟittukkoḷ
	 peṭṭiyil vaittukkoḷ.
 (3)	mukkōṇa vācalilē
	 muttuttaṭṭup pantalilē.
(4)	nāṉku ṭōṅku ṭammāram
	 nāṅkaḷāṭum pamparam.
(allatu)
	``nāṉku ṭōṅku
	 nāluveṟṟilai vāṅku
(5)	``aivar araikkum mañcaḷ 
	 tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ.
(6)	kūṟu kūṟu cittappā
	 kuṟukkē vanta periyappā.
(7)	ēḻai eṇṇik koḷ
	 eṇṇey maram cērttukkoḷ
	 peṇṇai aḻaittuk koḷ.  1
1.  ip pāṭṭiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷiṉ koccai vaṭivam tiruttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
ēḻāṅkoṭṭaikkup piṉ, oru kaiyil mukkallum iṉṉoru kaiyil nāṟkallumāka vaittukkoṇṭu, nāṟkalluḷ oṉṟai mēleṟintu eñciya irumūṉṟaiyuṅ kīḻvaittu mēleṟintu kallaip piṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum atai mēleṟintu ataik kīḻvaitta irumūṉṟaiyum irukaiyālum vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
itu ciṟuputai eṉappaṭum. itai āṭumpōtu pāṭum pāṭṭu putai putaikkiṟa pamparam, ceṭṭi citamparam eṉpatākum.
itaṉpiṉ, irukaiyilum mummūṉṟu kallai vaittuk koṇṭu, ēṉaiyoṉṟai mēleṟintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttup piṭittalvēṇṭum.
piṉpu, oru kallai mēleṟintu āṟukallaik kīḻ vaittup piṭittapiṉ, mīṇṭum oṉṟai mēleṟintu ēṉai āṟaiyum oruṅkē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu perumputai eṉappaṭum.
itaiyaṭuttut tappu - tāḷam - talaivali - mēḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum. piṉṉar oru kallaik kīḻiṭa, atai etiri eṭuttuk koṭuttal vēṇṭum.  itu paḻattiṉ oppakkuṟiyām.
IV. pala nāloru kal
oṉpatum patiṉmūṉṟum patiṉēḻum irupattoṉṟum pōl pala nāloṭu oṉṟucērnta kaṟkaḷai mēleṟintu, puṟaṅkaiyil tāṅkip, piṭikkumaḷavu vaittukkoṇṭu mikutiyaik kīḻiṭṭuviṭṭu, puṟaṅkaiyiluḷḷavaṟṟai mēlē pōṭṭu akaṅkaiyiṟ piṭittu, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu nannāṉkāy iṭakkaiyāṟ piṭittuk kīḻē nannāṉkāy vaittal vēṇṭum. ivvakaiyiṟ perumpālum oru nāṉkaittāṉ piṭittal kūṭum.
piṉpu, valakkaiyiluḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oru kallai mēlēṟintu ataik kīḻēyuḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiyō palavaṟṟaiyō eṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam kīḻē kalluḷḷa varai (allatu tavaṟum varai) tirumpat tirumpa āṭavēṇṭum.           kaiyiṟ palakaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭṭāl, uṭaṉē iṭakkaiyāl oru nāṉkai allatu pala nāṉkaip piṭittu nannāṉkāyk kīḻē vaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu ellāk kaṟkaḷaiyum         piṭittu nannāṉkāyk kīḻē vaittapiṉ, iṟutiyil eñciyuḷḷa oṟṟaikkallai mēlēpōṭṭup puṟaṅkaiyil tāṅki, atai mīṇṭum mēleṟintu nilattait toṭṭu, atu kīḻēviḻumuṉ ataik kaiyālaḻutti nērē vīḻtti mūṭiviṭal vēṇṭum. 
itu  amukkutal allatu mūṭutal eṉappaṭum. itōṭu oru paḻamām.
mutalāvatu puṟaṅkaiyāl tāṅkumpōtu ellāk kaṟkaḷaiyum kīḻē viṭṭuviṭṭālum, piṭikkumpōtu kal tavaṟiṉālum, nāṉkāy allatu nannāṉkāyp piṭikkumpōtu kūṭak kuṟaiyap piṭipaṭṭālum, kīḻuḷḷa kallai eṭukkumpōtu maṟṟak kal aluṅkiṉālum, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. piṉpu aṭuttavaḷ āṭavēṇṭum.
orē āṭṭaiyil, aṭuttavaḷ āṭiṉālum, āṭiṉavaḷē maṟumuṟai āṭiṉālum, nannāṉkāyp piṭittu vaikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu maṟṟak kaṟkaḷaik koṇṭutāṉ āṭavēṇṭum. orutti kaṭaicik kallai amukkumpōtu tavaṟippōy aṭuttavaḷ ataic cariyāy amukkiviṭṭāl, avaḷukkuttāṉ paḻam.
V. paṉṉiru kal
paṉṉiru kaṟkaḷai mēleṟintu avaṟṟaip puṟaṅkaiyil         tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaimaṭṭum iruviraṟ kiṭaiyil iṭukkikkoṇṭu ēṉaiyavaṟṟaik kīḻē viṭṭuviṭṭu,          avaṟṟai ovvoṉṟākavō ivviraṇṭākavō mummūṉṟā kavō, oṉṟum palavumākavō, vēṟiru viralāl iṭukkip           piṭittuk kīḻē vaittu ellāvaṟṟaiyum piṭittapiṉ puṟaṅkaiyi luḷḷatai amukki, ataiyum maṟṟavaṟṟoṭu cērttu           mummūṉṟāka nāṟkūṟiṭṭu, ovvoṉṟiṉiṉṟum ovvoru            kallai eṭuttuviṭavēṇṭum.  ik kūṟukaṭku uṭṭaikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. nāluṭṭaiyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru kallai nīkkiyapiṉ,           eṭṭuk kal eñci niṟkum. av veṭṭaiyum muṉpōṉṟē āṭi, mīṇṭum mummūṉṟāka uṭṭai vaittu ovvoru kallai          nīkkiyapiṉ, āṟu kal eñciniṟkum. ivvāṟē toṭarntu āṭiṉ, iṟutiyil irukal eñcum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai mēlēyeṟintu iṉṉoṉṟaik kīḻē vaittuppiṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum atai mēlēyeṟintu kīḻēvaittatai eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam mummuṟai ceytapiṉ, mēleṟinta kallai, mūṉṟu taṭavai ciluppiyum, mūṉṟu taṭavai mēlēṟintu nilantoṭṭup piṭittum, piṉṉum mūṉṟu taṭavai mēleṟintu nilamum mārpun toṭṭup piṭittum, muṭippiṉ paḻamām.
cilupputalāvatu, akaṅkaiyiluḷḷataip puṟaṅkaiyiliṭṭu veṭṭippiṭittal.
āṭumpōtu tavaṟum vakaiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻuñ ceytiyum, muṟkūṟiyavaiyē.
VI. pala kal
oṉpatu mutal irupattaintuvarai oṟṟaippaṭaiyāṉa ētēṉum oru tokaik kaṟkaḷai, mēlē pōṭṭup puṟaṅkaiyāl tāṅki orukal tavira maṟṟavaṟṟai yellāṅ kīḻē pōṭṭuviṭṭu, av voru kallai mēlēyeṟintu uḷḷaṅkaiyāṟ piṭittu, atai mīṇṭum mēlēyeṟintu, kīḻē kiṭakkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu eṉa iraṭṭaippaṭaiyāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu, mēlēyeṟinta kallaiyum piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr eṭuppiṟkum, muṉṉum piṉṉum, oru kallai mēleṟitalum ataip piṭittalum muṟaiyē nikaḻum.
irukal eṭuppiṉ kāy; nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu āyiṉ paḻam. paḻakkaṟkaḷellāvaṟṟaiyum taṉ paṅkil vaittukkoṇṭu, kāykkaṟkaḷiṟ pātiyai viḷaiyāṭṭiṟ pōṭṭuviṭalvēṇṭum. āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ, kūṭutalāṉa kaṟkaḷaip piṭittiruppavaḷ kelittavaḷāvaḷ.
piṟa iyalpukaḷum ceytikaḷum muṟkūṟiyavaiyē.
VII. patiṉāṟāṅ kal
patiṉāṟu kaṟkaḷaikkoṇṭu āṭuvatu patiṉāṟāṅkal. itu palavakaiyāy āṭappeṟum. vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār itai āṭuvar.
2. kiccuk kiccut tampalam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : tiriyai maṇṇuḷ vaittu maṟaittuk kiccuk kiccut tampalam eṉṟu colliyāṭum āṭṭu, ac collaiyē peyarākakkoṇṭatu. itu pāṇṭināṭṭil tirittiri pommutiri eṉa vaḻaṅkum. 
āṭuvār tokai : iru ciṟumiyar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oru muḻa nīḷamum nālviral uyaramumuḷḷa oru ciṟumaṇ allatu maṇaṟkaraiyum, oruviral akalamum iruviral nīḷamumuḷḷa oru tuṇittiriyum, itai āṭukaruviyām. tirikkup patilākac cilaviṭattuk kuccaiyum vaittukkoḷvatuṇṭu. potuvāka, pāṇṭināṭṭil tiriyum cōḻakoṅku nāṭṭil kuccum vaittuk koḷḷappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : maṇṇum maṇalum uḷḷa iṭamellām itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : orutti, tiriyaip paṟṟikkoṇṭirukkum taṉ valakkaip peruviral āṭkāṭṭiviralkaḷai maṇkaraiyiṉ valappakkattilum, veṟumaṉē poruttiyirukkum iṭakkaip peruviral āṭkāṭṭi viralkaḷai ataṉ iṭappakkattilum,           vaittu uṭpukutti ataṉ oru kōṭiyiṉiṉṟu maṟu kōṭivarai muṉṉum piṉṉumākap palamuṟai nakartti yiyakki, tiriyai           maṟaivāka ōriṭattil vaittuviṭṭup piṟitōriṭattil vaittatāka naṭittukkāṭṭi, tiriyuḷḷa viṭattiṟ kaivaikkumpaṭi taṉ         etiriyaik kēṭpāḷ. etiri taṉ irukaikaḷaiyum kōttut tāṉ aiyuṟṟaviṭattiṟ karaiyiṉ kuṟukkē potti vaippāḷ. avaḷ cariyāṉa iṭattilum vaittirukkalām; tavaṟāṉa iṭattilum vaittirukkalām. cariyāṉa iṭattil vaittiruntāl pottiṉa          vaḷum, tavaṟāṉa iṭattil vaittiruntāl tiriyai vaittavaḷum, veṉṟavarāvar. ovvoru veṟṟikkum aṭaiyāḷamāka         ovvoru ciṟumaṇ kuviyal vaikkappaṭum. yārāyiṉum, veṉṟavaḷē aṭutta muṟai āṭavēṇṭum.
tiriyai maṇṇukkuḷ vaittu maṟaikkumpōtu, kiccuk kiccut tampalam kīyāk kīyāt tampalam, maccu maccut tampalam māyā māyāt tampalam eṉṟu pāṭuvatuṇṭu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ kīḻvaru māṟu pāṭappaṭum :
tirittiri pommutiri
 tiri kālaṭi pommutiri
 kācu koṇṭu pommutiri
 kaṭaiyilē koṇṭum pommutiri
 nālu karaṇṭi nalleṇṇey
 nāṟpat toru tīvaṭṭi
 kaḷḷaṉ varukiṟāṉ katavaiyaṭai
 veḷḷacci varukiṟāḷ viḷakkēṟṟu
 varukiṟār aiyā cuppaiyā
 vaḻivi ṭammā mīṉāṭci.
itil, varukiṟāṉ varukiṟāḷ varukiṟār eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, muṟaiyē, vāṟāṉ, vāṟāḷ, vāṟār eṉak koccai vaṭiviṟ pāṭappaṭum.
pattumuṟai munti veṉṟavaḷ, etiriyiṉ kūṭṭukkai nirampa maṇṇaḷḷi vaittu ataṉuḷ tiriyai (allatu kuccai) vaittu maṟaittu, maṇ naṭuvil eccait tuppi atil oru ciṟṟeṟumpaip piṭittup pōṭṭu, etiriyiṉ kaṇpaṭṭaimayir allatu talaimayir oṉṟai avaḷaip piṭuṅkaccolli ataiyum av         vecciṉmēl iṭṭu, avaḷ kaṇṇai iṟukappotti aimpatu kacattolaivu koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇṇaik kīḻē          koṭṭuvittu, piṉpu puṟappaṭṭa iṭattiṟkuk koṇṭuvantu, kaṇṇaip pottiṉa kaiyai eṭuttuviṭṭu, maṇ koṭṭiṉa iṭattiṟkup pōyt tiriyai (allatu kuccai) eṭuttukkoṇaṭu varaccolvāḷ. etiri eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantuviṭṭāl taṇṭaṉaiyillai; illāviṭiṉ avaḷ talaiyil oru kuṭṭuk kuṭṭappaṭum. etiri maṇ koṭṭiya iṭattai eḷitāyk kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyātapaṭi, avaḷaik kaṇpottik koṇṭupōmpōtum koṇṭu varumpōtum, nērākac ceṉṟu nērāka varāmal vaḷaintu vaḷaintu pala ticaiyil pōyvaruvatu vaḻakkam. veṉṟavaḷ tōṟṟavaḷaik kuṭṭumpōtu, pakkattilirukkum piḷḷaikaḷum āḷukkoṉṟu kuṭṭuvatu cilaviṭattuṇṭu.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl itu koḷḷait toḻili ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : niṉaivāṟṟalum ticaiyaṟiyun tiṟaṉum itaṉāl vaḷarkkap peṟalām.
3. kuṟiñci (kuñci)
potuvāka aivarkkuk kuṟaiyāta pala piḷḷaikaḷ varicaiyākavō vaṭṭamākavō kūṭṭamākavō niṉṟu koṇṭu, avaruḷ orutti ēṉai ovvoruttiyaiyum varicaip paṭi cuṭṭi, oṇṇari, ṭūvari, ṭikkari, āvaṉ, cāvaṉ, iṅkilīṣ, meṉ, piḷauṉ, pōṭiṅ, ciṭ eṉṟu colluvāḷ. ciṭ eṉṟa collappaṭṭa piḷḷai nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, mīṇṭum mīṇṭum pala taṭavai ic coṟkaḷ collappaṭum. ovvoru taṭavaiyum ciṭ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa piḷḷai nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu ceyyiṉ iṟutiyil oru piḷḷai eñciniṟkum. avaḷ maṟṟavarai ōṭit toṭavēṇṭum.
āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ kālāl oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum. toṭavēṇṭiyavaḷ piṟarellām ōṭippōy oḷintu koḷḷumvarai, taṉ kaṇṇai mūṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ellārum oḷintukoṇṭapiṉ varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, avar varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ, toṭavēṇṭiyavaḷ aṅkumiṅkum ōṭippōyp palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭippārppāḷ. oḷintiruntavar ōṭivantu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟuviṭiṉ, avarait toṭal kūṭātu. vaṭṭam nōkki ōṭivarumpōtu, āṭai aviḻntuviṭṭālum kīḻē viḻuntuviṭṭālum, aṉṟum avarait toṭalkūṭātu. cilar, oḷintirāmalē nīṇṭanēram āṭṭamkāṭṭi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vantu niṉṟukoḷvar.
vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē toṭappaṭṭavaḷ, aṭutta muṟai muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṟarait toṭalvēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattiṟkup patilāka ōr ellaipporuḷ kuṟikkappaṭu vatumuṇṭu. av vellaip poruḷait toṭṭuviṭṭālum, ataṉpiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu.
(2) iravāṭṭu
1. pākkuveṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pākkuveṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkum viḷaiyāṭṭu, ac collaiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu; itu vaṭakoṅku nāṭṭil paruppuccaṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār tokai : potuvāka aṟuvarkkuk kuṟaiyāta palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : ūrppoṭṭal itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : talaimaiyāṉa iru petumpaiyār1 aṇṇāviyarpōl etiretir niṉṟukoḷvar. avaruḷ oruttiyiṉ piṉṉāl, ēṉaic ciṟumiyarellārum orutti araiyāṭaiyai iṉṉorutti  paṟṟikkoṇṭu varicaiyāy niṟpar. iṉṉorutti, av varicaikku etir niṉṟu maṟukkāṭṭi valamum iṭamum cuṟṟicceṉṟu, varicaiyāy niṟkum ciṟumiyaruḷ aṇṇāviyoḻinta piṟaruḷ oruttiyai allatu palarait toṭamuyalvāḷ. avaḷ valañ cellum pōtu iṭamum, iṭañ cellum pōtu valamumāka, varicaiyāy niṟkuñ ciṟumiyar vaḷaintu vaḷaintu iyaṅkuvar. toṭappaṭṭa peṇ nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam (aṇṇāviyoḻinta) ellāp peṇkaḷum toṭappaṭumvarai, āṭṭut toṭaralām.
ōr āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ maṟumuṟaiyum muṉ pōṉṟē āṭappeṟum.
āṭṭu nikaḻumpōtu, taṉittu niṟpavaḷum varicai mutalviyumāṉa aṇṇāviyar iruvarum, piṉ varumāṟu
1.  eṭṭu akavai mutal 11 akavai varaiyuḷḷa peṇ petumpai.
pāṭṭuppāṭi neṭukalum uṟaḻnturaippar. pāṭṭu muṭintavuṭaṉ tiruppappaṭum.
pāṇṭināṭṭup pāṭṭu
(1)	ta :	pākkuveṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē.
	va :	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
(2)	ta :	veṟṟilaip peṭṭiyaik kāṇōmē.
	va :	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
(3)	ta :	āṭukiṭakkiṟa kiṭaiyaip pār.
	va :	āṭṭup piḻukkaiyait tūrttup pār.
(4)	ta :	kuṭṭi kiṭakkiṟa kiṭaiyaip pār.
	va :	kuṭṭip piḻukkaiyait tūrttup pār.
(5)	ta :	pallē valikkutē
	va :	nellaik koṟittukkoḷ.
koṅkunāṭṭup pāṭṭu
(1)	ta :	paruppuc caṭṭi.
	va :	tiruppi nakku.1
(2)	ta :	vāḻai yilai.
	va :	vaḻittu nakku.2
(3)	ta :	ūciyālē kuttuvēṉ.
	va :	vīṭṭumēlē ēṟuvēṉ.
(4)	ta :	kiṇaṟṟilē kutippēṉ.
	va :	kalleṭuttup pōṭuvēṉ.
(5)	ta :	talaiyē nōkutē.
	va :	talaiyaṇai pōṭṭukkoḷ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : inta āṭṭu, nari āṭṭukkuṭṭi kaḷaiyō, paruntu kōḻik kuñcukaḷaiyō, piṭippatiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
1,2.	`nakku eṉṉuñ coṟkup patilāka `nokku eṉṟu vaittukkoḷḷalām. nokkutal = uṇṭu kuṟaiyac ceytal, mikutiyāka vuṇṇutal.
2. nilāk kuppal
āṭuvārellārum 1ām kaṭci 2ām kaṭci eṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar.
piṉpu, iru kaṭciyārum vevvēṟu pakkamākac ceṉṟu, kuṟippiṭṭa ellaikkuḷ, maraniḻalum teruccantum poṭṭalum pōṉṟa pala iṭaṅkaḷil āḷukkoṉṟākac ciṟuciṟu 
maṇ kuppalkaḷai vaittuviṭṭu vantuviṭuvar. maṇ 
kuppalukku aṭaiyāḷamāka mēlē kuccu naṭṭuvaippatumuṇṭu. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇ kuppalukkup patilākat teṉṉaimaram varaivar.
iru kaṭciyārum kuppal vaittuviṭṭuvanta piṉ, ovvoru kaṭciyārum oruṅkē ceṉṟu, etirk kaṭciyār vaitta kuppalkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittu aḻittuviṭuvar. mutalāvatu, oṉṟāṅ kaṭciyār iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyāriṉ kuppalkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭippar. ellāk kuppalkaḷaiyum kaṇṭupiṭippatu perumpālum aritākum. kaṇṭupiṭittavaṟṟai aḻittapiṉ, vayiṟu nirampiviṭṭata  eṉṟu   2ām kaṭciyār kēṭpar. mēluṅ kaṇṭupiṭikka viruppamiruppiṉ, iṉṉum vayiṟu nirampavillai eṉṟum, viruppamillāviṭiṉ,  vayiṟu nirampiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, oṉṟāṅ kaṭciyār patiluraippar. piṉpu, 2ām kaṭciyār 1ām kaṭciyār kaṇṭupiṭiyāta kuppal          kaḷaik kāṭṭi avaṟṟai eṇṇikkoḷvar. ivvāṟē 2ām kaṭciyār 1ām kaṭciyāriṉ kuppalkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtum nikapam. iṅṅaṉam iru kaṭciyārum kaṇṭupiṭittu muṭintapiṉ, ekkaṭciyār mikutiyāṉa kuppalkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittiruk            kiṉṟaṉarō, ak kaṭciyār tam kaṭciyai aracaṉ (rājā) eṉṟu collik koḷvar. iṅṅaṉam palamuṟai āṭi, 50 allatu 
100 eṉak kuṟitta eṇṇikkaiyai muntiyaṭainta kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar. tōṟṟa kaṭciyār tōppukkaraṇam pōṭal         vēṇṭum. kuppalukkup patilākat teṉṉaimaram varaiyappaṭiṉ, ataṉ ovvoru kōṭṭiṟkum eṇṇikkaiyuṇṭu.
maṟaikkappaṭṭataik kaṇṭupiṭikkum payiṟci iv viḷai yāṭṭiluṇṭu eṉalām. inta āṭṭuc ciṟumiyarukkuriyatāyiṉum, aintāṇṭiṟkum pattāṇṭiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa ciṟuvarum itiṟ kalantukoḷvar.
3. paṉṉīrkkuḷattil muḻukutal
patiṉoru peṇpiḷḷaikaḷ kūṭit teruvil ōriṭattil vaṭṭamāy  uṭkārntapiṉ, avaruḷ talaimaiyāṉavaḷ ovvoruttiyiṉ muṭṭikkālkaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟāka varicaippaṭi cuṟṟic cuṟṟit toṭṭukkoṇṭu.
	1	2	3	4
	oruppuṭṭam	tiruppuṭṭam	ōṭivā	maṅkaḷam
	5	6	7	8
	cekkait	tiruppic	cevveṇṇey	vārttu
	9	10	11	12
	māṭuṅ	kaṉṟum	varukiṟa	vēḷai
	13	14	15	16
	mañcaḷ	taṇṇīr	teḷikkiṟa	vēḷai
	17	18	19
	kālai	maṭakkaṭi	kāmāṭci
eṉṉum maraput toṭarai, toṭukaikkoṉṟākap pattoṉpatu cīrpaṭac colvāḷ. kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ uṭaṉē oru kālai maṭakki uṭkāra vēṇṭum (atāvatu maṇṭiyiṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum). iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum am maraput toṭar collappaṭṭu, ovvoru taṭavaiyum kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ taṉ kālai maṭakkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭān taṭavaiyākak kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭiyum peṇ taṉ maṟu kālaiyum maṭakkiṉavuṭaṉ eḻuntu pōyviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ēṉaip patiṉmarum eḻuntupōy ōriṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāyiruppar.
piṉpu, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ ōriṭattil taṉimaiyāka iruntukoṇṭu, mutalil pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉpāḷ. avaḷ vantavuṭaṉ uṉ kuḻantaiyai eṉṉa ceytāy? eṉṟu kēṭpāḷ. avaḷ, eṉ kuḻantaiyaik kiṇaṟṟil muṟittup pōṭṭuviṭṭuk kuḷattil muḻukiviṭṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu colvāḷ. nī entak kuḷattil muḻukiṉāy? eṉṟu talaimai yāṉavaḷ kēṭpāḷ. ataṟku avaḷ, tayirkkuḷattil muḻuki ṉēṉ eṉṟu patiluraippāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām veḷḷaiyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colli, avaḷait turattiviṭuvāḷ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā, mūṉṟāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā, eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eñciyōruḷ ovvoruttiyaiyum muṟaiyē aḻaittu muṉpōṟ kēṭpāḷ. ovvoru taṭavaiyum muṉ coṉṉavāṟē nikaḻum. kuḷattil muḻukiṉataippaṟṟic collumpōtu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci paruppukkuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. aṉṟu, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām mañcaḷāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turatti viṭuvāḷ. mūṉṟāvatupōṉa kāmāṭci neyk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām eṇṇeyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu, collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. nālāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci pavvīk1 kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talaimaiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām pavvīyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu peṇkaḷ ēṟkāta ovvoṉṟaic collit turattappaṭṭapiṉ, pattāvatu peṇmaṭṭum nāṉ paṉṉīrk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē talaimaiyāṉavaḷ vā vā vā eṉṟu colli avaḷaic cērttu aṇaittuk koḷvāḷ. atōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
talaimaiyāṉavaḷiṭam ovvoruttiyum ōṭi varum pōtu, ōr iṭakkarāṉa marapurai kūṟikkoṇṭu varuvatu vaḻakkam.
1.  iṭakkarāṉa col iṭakkaraṭakki acciṭappeṟṟatu.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
1. orukuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi
 āṭṭiṉ peyar : orukuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi eṉṟu toṭaṅkum pāṭṭaip pāṭi āṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu, am mutaṟ kuṟippaiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭuvār tokai : itai āṭa nālvarkkuk kuṟaiyātu vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : itu teruvil āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟai : iruvar kaikōttu uyartti vaittuk koṇṭirukka avarukkiṭaiyē vēṟiruvar allatu palar oruttipiṉ oruttiyāka orutti yaraiyāṭaiyai iṉṉorutti paṟṟikkoṇṭu nuḻaintu ceṉṟu, oru kuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi oru pūp pūttatām, iraṇṭu kuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi iraṇṭu pūp pūttatām eṉṟu pattukkuṭam taṇṇīr varaiyum pāṭik koṇṭu kaikōttu niṟkum iruvaraiyum māṟi māṟic cuṟṟik koṇṭēyiruppar.
pattukkuṭam taṇṇīr ūṟṟi eṉṟa aṭimuṭintavuṭaṉ, allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpē, kaikōttu niṟkum iruvarum kaiyait tāḻtti iraṇṭāvatu allatu kaṭaicip piḷḷaiyaip piṭittukkoḷvar allatu ciṟaiceyvar. appōtu varicai mutalvikkum kaikōttu niṟpavarukkum piṉvarumāṟu uṟaḻurai yāṭṭu nikaḻum.
va :	viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(kaṇaikkāliṟ kaivaittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ1, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va : 	(muḻaṅkālil kaivaittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(iṭuppiṟ kaivaittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(tōḷiṟ kaivaittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(talaiyiṟ kai vaittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(talaikkumēṟ kaitūkki) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
va :	(talaikkumēṟ kaitūkkik kutittu) immām immām poṉ tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā!
kai :	viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā!
iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻnturaiyāṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭuk kaṟiyum cōṟum tarukiṟēṉ, viṭaṭā tulukkā! eṉṟu varicai mutalvi kūṟiyavuṭaṉ, ciṟaiceyyappaṭṭa piḷḷai viṭutalai ceyyap paṭum. atōṭu viḷaiyāṭṭu muṭiyum. viṭaṭā tulukkā! eṉṉum ēvalum, viṭamāṭṭēṉ malukkā eṉṉum maṟuppum, orumai kuṟittaṉavēṉum, kaikōttu niṟpavar iruvarum oruṅkē ēvappaṭuvatum oruṅkē maṟutturaippatumē marapām.
1.	`poṉ tarukiṟēṉ eṉpatu `poṉṉu tāṟēṉ eṉṟu koccai vaṭiviṟ collappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : araṇmaṉaip pūntōṭṭattiṟkuk kāvalāyirunta oru makamatiyaṉ, taṉ kāval tōṭṭattil pūppaṟittatoru piḷḷaiyaip piṭittukkoṇṭu, avaḷ peṟṟōr evvaḷavu paṇantantum ēṟkāmal kaṭaiciyil pulavu allatu kaṟiccōṟu taruvatākac coṉṉavuṭaṉ, ap piḷḷaiyai viṭṭuviṭṭa ceytiyai, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatupōl uḷḷatu iv viḷaiyāṭṭu.
2. eṉ ulakkai kuttuk kuttu
āṭu muṟai : irupatu ciṟumiyarpōl oru vīṭṭu muṟṟattil vaṭṭamāka neruṅki yiruntu, naṭuvaṇ (maiyam) nōkkik kālnīṭṭi orutti pātattōṭu orutti pātattaic cērttuvaittu, ovvorut tiyum iru muḻaṅkaiyālum iru vilāvaiyum iṭaiviṭātu aṭittukkoṇṭum, eṉ ulakkai kuttuk kuttu, akkā ulakkai cantaikkup pō eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkip pāṭikkoṇṭu, mella mellap peyarntu cuṟṟic cuṟṟi iyaṅkikkoṇṭē yiruppar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : tamakkaik koṉṟum taṅkaik koṉṟumākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa īrulakkaikaḷuḷ, tamakkaiyiṉatu cantaikku viṟpaṉaikkākap pōka, taṅkaiyiṉatu taṅkippōṉa tiṉāl, avaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu ataṉāl kuttiṉa ceyalai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu niṉaivu kūrvikkiṉṟatu pōlum!
3. ūtā maṇi
pala piḷḷaikaḷuḷ mūvar kaikōttuc cāṭ pūṭtiri eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu mūṉṟutaram kai acaittut tiri eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ kaiyai yuruvik kaimēṟkai vaippar. mēṟkai, akaṅkai mēṉōkkiyiruntāl veḷḷai eṉṟum, puṟaṅkai mēṉōkkiyiruntāl karuppu eṉṟum, collappaṭum. iv viraṇṭil evvakaiyilēṉum, taṉiyāka vaitta piḷḷai vilakiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, ēṉaiyiruvarum mutaliṟ cērāta vēṟoru piḷḷaiyuṭaṉ kaikōttu, muṉpōṉṟē oru piḷḷaiyaip pirikkavēṇṭum. mūvarum orē vakaiyāyk kaivaittiruntāl, mīṇṭuṅ kōkkavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam mummūvarāyk kaikōttu ovvoruttiyaip pirittapiṉ, iṟutiyil iruvar iruppar. av viruvaruṭaṉ, mutaṉmutaṟ pirinta piḷḷai oppukku eṉṟu collik kaikōkkum. muṉpu piriyāta iruvaruḷ yārēṉum orutti taṉivakaiyiṟ kaivaippiṉ, avaḷ vilakiviṭa, aṭuttavaḷ akappaṭṭukkoḷvāḷ.
akappaṭṭuk koṇṭavaḷ caṟṟut tolaiviṟpōy niṟka, avaḷukku muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṇimālai allatu vēṟōr aṇi, oru piḷḷaiyiṭam oḷittu vaikkappaṭum. tolaivil niṟpavaḷ, varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ vantu, maṇimālai vaittirukkiṟavaḷ eṉṟu oru piḷḷaiyai ūkippāyc cuṭṭikkāṭṭum. illātavaḷ eḻuntu niṟpāḷ. aiyattiṟkiṭamāyiruppiṉ, avaḷ maṭi mukaliyavaṟṟaip piṭittup pārkkalām. orumuṟai kaṇṭupiṭikkat tavaṟiṉavaḷ maṟumuṟaiyuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. maṇimālai vaittiruppavaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuviṭiṉ, maṟṟellāp piḷḷaikaḷuñ cērntu, ūtāmaṇiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittāl kaitaṭṭuvōm eṉṟu pāṭik kaitaṭṭi makiḻvar. itu teruvilum ūrppoṭṭalilum viḷaiyāṭappeṟum.
4. pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm
potuvākap patiṉmarukkuk kuṟaiyāta pala piḷḷaikaḷ uttikaṭṭic camamāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu, ōr akaṉṟa teruvilēṉum ūrppoṭṭalilēṉum iru kaṭcikkum potuvāka oru naṭukkē ṭu kīṟi, ataṟku ippālum appālum niṉṟukoḷvar. piṉpu irukaṭciyārum kaṭcivāriyākak kaikōttu muṉpiṉṉākap piṉ varumāṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, māṟimāṟi naṭukkōṭu varaiceṉṟu mīḷvar.
mutaṟ kaṭciyār : pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm,
	pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm, inta nāḷilē.
iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyār : yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr?
	yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? inta nāḷilē.
mu :	kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm,
	  kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm, intanāḷilē.
i :	entappū vēṇṭum?
	  entappū vēṇṭum? inta nāḷilē.
mu :	mallikaippū vēṇṭum,
	  mallikaippū vēṇṭum, inta nāḷilē.
iṉṉapū vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mutaṟkaṭciyār pāṭi muṭintatum , irukaṭciyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru piḷḷai muṉceṉṟu naṭukkōṭṭai yaṭuttavuṭaṉ, iruvarum oruttiyai yorutti piṭittiḻuppar. kōṭṭiṟkappāl iḻuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭa piḷḷai etirkkaṭciyaic cērum. piṉpu muṉpōṟ pāṭi, vēṟiruvar iḻuppar. iṅṅaṉam ivviruvarāy ellārum iḻukkappaṭṭapiṉ, mikutiyākap piḷḷaikaḷ cērntirukkiṟa kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar.
5. taṇṇīr cēntukiṟatu
nāṉku piḷḷaikaḷ, caturamāka neruṅki oṟṟaik kālāl niṉṟu maṟṟaik kālaik kālmēṟpōṭṭu, naṭuviṟ kiṇaṟu pōṉṟa paḷḷam tōṟṟi,
taṇṇīr cēntit taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟu, 
 pūppaṟittup pūppaṟittuk kūṭaiyilē pōṭu, 
 viṟṟuviṟṟup paṇattaiyeṭuttup peṭṭiyilē pōṭu,
eṉṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟuvatupōṉṟum, pūppaṟittuk kūṭaiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, pū viṟṟa paṇattaip peṭṭiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, avvav vaṭikkēṟpa avinayañ ceyvar.
oṟṟaik kālil niṟkum payiṟciyai iv viḷaiyāṭṭāṟ peṟalām.
irupāṟ pakuti
ciṟuvarum ciṟumiyarum pāl vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalantum, paiyaṉkaḷum petumpaiyarum pāl vēṟupāṭṭāṟ pirintum, āṭum āṭṭut tokuti irupāṟ pakutiyām.
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. paṇṇāṅkuḻi
I. potuvakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : neṟkuttum paṇṇaipōl vaṭṭamāṉa paḷḷam allatu kuḻitōṇṭi, atiṟ kaṟkaḷaiyiṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉappaṭum. paṇṇaiyeṉpatu paḷḷam. paṇṇai paṟittal kuḻitōṇṭutal.
paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉṉum peyar, avvav viṭattaip poṟuttup paṉṉāṅkuḻi, pallāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉa vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkum. perumpālum patiṉāṉku kuḻivaittu iv viḷaiyāṭṭu āṭappeṟuvatāl, patiṉāṉku kuḻi eṉpatu muṟaiyē patiṉāṅkuḻi paṉṉāṅkuḻi eṉat tirintatākac cilar koḷvar. āyiṉ, patiṉāṅkuḻi eṉa eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅkāmaiyāṉum, paṉṉāṉku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakkātalā ṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷē peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, patiṉāṉkiṟkuk kuṟaintum kūṭiyum kuḻikaḷ vaittuk koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpataṟkup paḷḷamāṉa kuḻi eṉṟē potumakkaḷāṟ poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi allatu paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpatē tiruntiya vaṭivamām.
āṭuvār tokai : itai iruvar āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : nilattiṟ camamāṉa irupaṭukkai varicaiyākak killappaṭṭa 10 allatu 14 allatu 16 kuḻikaḷum, avaṟṟuḷ avvaintāy iṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya kaḻaṟcikkāy (kaccakkāy) allatu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭai allatu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum, itaṟku vēṇṭuṅ karuvikaḷām.
cilar, eṉṟum eṅkum vacatiyāy āṭutaṟporuṭṭu, vēṇṭiya aḷavu paḷḷañ cetukkappeṟṟa marakkaṭṭaikaḷai vaittiruppar.
āṭiṭam : itu vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭu allatu maraniḻalilum āṭappeṟum.  itu ēṉai vakaikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟai : kuḻi varicaikkoruvarāka iruvar varicai yaṭuttu etiretir uṭkārntu, kuḻikkaintāka ellāk kuḻikaḷilum kaṟkaḷaip pōṭuvar. muntiyāṭupavar, tam varicaiyil ētēṉumoru kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaintaiyum eṭuttu, valappuṟamākac cuṟṟik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭukkoṇṭē pōtal vēṇṭum. kaṟkaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭaicikkal pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaintaiyum eṭuttu, ataṟkappāluḷḷa kuḻiyil ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōtalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam pōṭumpōtu, tam varicaiyil iṭavalamākavum, etiri varicaiyil vala iṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi, veṟumaiyāka iruntāl ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaṉaittaiyum eṭuttut tammiṭam vaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, etiriyār tam varicaiyiluḷḷa oru kuḻiyiliruntu toṭaṅki, muṉ coṉṉavāṟē āṭuvar. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻiyilum kal illāviṭiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum tattam varicaiyiluḷḷa kuḻiyoṉṟil 4 kaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭiṉ, avaṟṟaip pacu eṉṟu colli eṭuttukkoḷvar. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, 8 kal cērntuviṭiṉ avaṟṟaip paḻam eṉṟu colli eṭuttukkoḷvar.
iṅṅaṉam iruvarum māṟi māṟi āṭivarumpōtu, eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭātu eñciyuḷḷa kaṟkaḷellām orē varicaiyiṟ pōyc cērntuviṭiṉ, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. iruvarum tāntām eṭuttuvaittirukkum kaṟkaḷai eṇṇuvar. kaṭaiciyil orē varicaiyiṟ pōyc cērnta kaṟkaḷellām, av varicaiyāraic cērum.  mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷai eṭuttavar veṉṟavarāvar.
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, orē āṭṭaiyil veṟṟiyait tīrmāṉiyāmal, aintāṭṭaiyiṉ piṉ allatu pattāṭṭaiyiṉ piṉ tīrmāṉippar. ammuṟaippaṭi āṭumpōtu, muṉ āṭṭaikaḷil tōṟṟavar veṉṟavariṭam tamakku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ vāṅkik koḷvar. veṉṟavar kaṭaṉ koṭāviṭiṉ, irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷai avvaintākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta allatu aintu kal illāta kuḻikaḷai veṟumaiyāka viṭṭuviṭavēṇṭum. av veṟum kuḻikaṭkup pavvīk kuḻikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. avaṟṟil ovvoru kuccu iṭappaṭum.  oruvarum avaṟṟil āṭal kūṭātu.
tōṟṟavarkku orukuḻiyum nirampātapōtu (atāvatu aintu kaṟkum kuṟaivāka irukkumpōtu) irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷai ovvoṉṟākak kuḻikaḷiṟ pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta kuḻikaḷaip pavvīk kuḻikaḷāka viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu etiriyārum tam varicaiyiluḷḷa ellāk kuḻikaṭkum ovvoru kallē pōṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru kallē pōṭṭu āṭum muṟaikkuk kañci kāyccutal eṉṟu peyar. avvaintu kal pōṭṭu āṭit tōṟṟavar, kañcikāycci āṭumpōtu vella iṭamuṇṭu. oru muṟai veṉṟavar maṟumuṟai muntiyāṭal 
vēṇṭum.
ōr āṭṭai muṭintapiṉ, veṟṟiyum tōlviyumiṉṟi iruvarum camamākak kaṟkaḷ vaittiruppiṉ, aṭutta āṭṭaiyil caripāṇṭi ā ṭal vēṇṭum. mutalāvatu avvaintākavum piṉpu pappattākavum kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu, attaṉaiyē pōṭṭu āṭuvatu, caripāṇṭiyāṭalākum. itil viraintu viḷaivu kāṇalām.
II. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nāṉku mūlaikkuṅ kaṭṭukkaṭṭi āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷaiyāṭṭuvakai, kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār tokai : iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : iru camavaricaiyākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkaintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭakar ovvoruvarum tattam varicaiyiṉ iṭakkōṭi (atāvatu iṭappuṟak kaṭaici)k kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. eṭutta kaṟkaḷaiyellām ovvoṉṟāyp pōṭṭapiṉ, kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu āṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē oru kallēṉum iruntāltāṉ, atiṉiṉṟu eṭukka muṭiyum; illāviṭṭāl (atāvatu pōṭṭapiṉ oru kallēyiruntāl) eṭukka muṭiyātu. veṟuṅkuḻiyait tuṭaittu aṭutta kuḻiyiliruntēṉum, piṟa vakaiyilēṉum, kaṟkaḷai eṭuttu vaittukkoḷḷutal iv viḷaiyāṭṭil illai. oru mūlaik kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē mūṉṟu kalliruppiṉ, nālāvatu kallaip pōṭumpōtu, ataik kuḻikkuḷ pōṭāmal kuḻikkup pakkamāka vaittuviṭa vēṇṭum. ituvē kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭuvatu. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil, kaṭṭiṉavarē mēṟkoṇṭu kalpōṭa muṭiyum; aṭuttavar pōṭamuṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam oruvarēyō iruvarumā kavō nāṉku mūlaikkum kaṭṭuk kaṭṭik koḷḷalām. kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil kal pōṭalāmēyaṉṟi eṭukka muṭiyātu. ātalāl, kaṭṭukkaṭṭiya kuḻikaḷil mēlum mēlum kal cērntu koṇṭē yirukkum. ellāk kaṟkaḷum nāṉku mūlaikkum pōyc cērntapiṉ avaravar kaṭṭiya kuḻikkaṟkaḷai yeṭuttu eṇṇal vēṇṭum. mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyavar veṉṟa varāvar.
III. aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum eṉa mūvar āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvakai, avar puṉaipatavip peyaraiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭukkaṭṭaṟkup pōṉṟē, iru camavaricai yākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkaintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itai āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟai : iru varicaik kuḻikaḷuḷḷum, naṭu iru mūṉṟu aracaṉuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟum, iṭappuṟa īriraṇṭu mantiriyuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟum, valappuṟa īriraṇṭu cēvakaṉuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟum koṇṭu, aracaṉ mantiri cēvakaṉ eṉṟa varicaiyoḻuṅkil, āṭakar mūvarum āṭal vēṇṭum.
ovvoruvarum, tāntām āṭum ovvoru taṭavaiyum,        tattam kuḻiyiliruntē toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. āyiṉ, ellār kuḻikaḷilum varicaippaṭi kaṟkaḷaip pōṭalām. ovvoṉ        ṟākak kaṟkaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭinta piṉ, kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa  kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaiyeṭuttu muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi veṟumaiyāka iruppiṉ atait tuṭaittu ataṟkaṭutta         kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaiyellām eṭuttu vaittukkoḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭalvēṇṭum. kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻikaḷum veṟumaiyāka iruppiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. veṟuṅkuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷai eṭukkumpōtu, yārum yār kāyaiyum eṭukkalām.
avaravar kuḻikaḷiluḷḷa pacuvai avaravar eṭuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
ellāk kāyum eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa piṉ, aracaṉ ēṉaiyiruvarkkum ovvoṉṟu koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, avaṉukku 6 kuḻi nirampiṉāl (atāvatu 30 kāy iruntāl), avaṉukku veṟṟiyām. eṉṟum, yār mikunta kāy eṭukkiṉṟaṉarō avarukkē veṟṟiyām. veṉṟavar tamakkaṭutta mēṟpataviyum tōṟṟavar tamakkaṭutta kīḻppataviyum aṭaital vēṇṭum. aracaṉ velliṉ taṉ pataviyaik kāttuk koḷvāṉ. āyiṉ, aracaṉum mantiriyum mūṉṟumuṟai toṭarntu paḻamāyiṉ, kīḻppatavi yaṭaitalvēṇṭum. ulaka vāḻkkaiyi luḷḷa ēṟṟiṟakkam iv viḷaiyāṭṭāl uṇarttappaṭuvatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
IV. acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar tamittirukkumpōtu tāmāy āṭikkoḷḷam paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvakai, acōka vaṉattil taṉittiruntatākac collappaṭum cītaiyoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭuk kaṭṭaṟkuriyavaiyē itaṟkum.
āṭumuṟai : irukuḻi varicaiyuḷḷum, mēlvaricaiyil, muṟaiyē oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintum āṟum ēḻumāka mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟaiyilum, kīḻvaricaiyil, muṟaiyē ēḻum āṟum aintum nāṉkum mūṉṟum iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka mēṉmē liṟakka muṟaiyilum, iṭavalamākak kāykaḷaip pōṭṭukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, mēl varicaiyil mēṉmē liṟakka muṟaiyilum kīḻvaricaiyil mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟaiyilum pōṭṭukkoḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, iruvaricai yilum ēṟṟiṟakka muṟai māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē vitiyām.
toṭaṅkumpōtu kīḻvaricaiyiluḷḷa peruntokaik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu (atāvatu 7 kāykaḷ uḷḷa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu) toṭaṅ kal vēṇṭum. kāykaḷaik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuc cellumpōtu, piṟavakai yāṭṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē kīḻvaricai yil iṭavalamākavum mēl varicaiyil valaiṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kāykaḷaip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭaici yākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta peruntokaik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kāykaḷai eṭuttāṭavēṇṭum. atu kaṭaiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭuttē yirukkum.
veṟuṅkuḻi amaivatum, atait tuṭaittu aṭutta kuḻi
yiluḷḷa kāykaḷai eṭuttukkoḷvatum, iv viḷaiyāṭṭil illai.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu (atāvatu eṉṟum oru varicaiyi luḷḷa peruntokaik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kāyeṭuttu) oṉpatu muṟai toṭarntu āṭiṉ, āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭṭirunta vāṟē, avvak kuḻikaḷiṟ kāykaḷ amaintuviṭum. aṅṅaṉam irāviṭiṉ āṭiṉatu tavaṟeṉṟu aṟintukoḷḷalām.
oruvar tamittāṭutaṟkēṟṟa viruppattai iv viḷaiyāṭṭu uṇṭupaṇṇa vallatu.
kuṟippu :	paṇṇāṅkuḻi peṇpiḷḷaikaṭkē ēṟṟatāyiṉum, paiyaṉ kaḷālum āṭappaṭuvatupaṟṟi irupāṟ pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭateṉka.
2. pāṇṭi
(1) pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷḷatāy nilattiṟ kīṟap paṭṭa araṅkiṉuḷ vaṭṭeṟintu, atai noṇṭiyaṭittuk kālāl taḷḷiyāṭum āṭṭu, pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
iv viḷaiyāṭṭu, ēṉaiyiru tamiḻnāṭukaḷilum, vaṭṭu eṉṟum cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum eṟikaruviyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatāl, orukāl pāṇṭi eṉpatum eṟikaruvip peyarāy irukkalām.
pāṇṭil eṉṉuñ col vaṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, ataṉ kaṭaikkuṟaiyāṉa pāṇṭi eṉpatum ap poruḷ paṭalām. vaṭṭu cilli (cillākku) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, vaṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷaiyē moḻip poruṭ kāraṇamākak koṇṭiruppatu pōl, pāṇṭi eṉpatum koṇ ṭirukkalām.
pāṇṭi viḷaiyāṭṭiṟku vaṭṭu allatu vaṭṭāṭṭu eṉpatu paḻam peyar. iṉṟum paḻañcēra nāṭākiya malaiyāḷattil ap peyarē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṭuvār tokai : itai iruvar āṭuvar. itu ēṉai vakaikaṭkum okkum.
āṭukaruvi : varicaikku mūṉṟu āka iru naṭṭu varicaiyāka āṟu kaṭṭaṅkoṇṭa oru nīḷcatura araṅkum, āṭakar ovvoruvarkkum araiyaṅkulak kaṉamum īraṅkula viṭṭamumuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamāṉa kal allatu ōṭṭāñcalliyum, itai āṭu karuviyām. araṅku, potuvāka, āṟaṭi nīḷamum nālaṭi akalamum uḷḷatāyum, mēṟkuṟumpakkattil, camuttiram eṉṉum ōr araivaṭṭaṅ koṇṭatāyum, irukkum. av varai vaṭṭattiṉ ucciyil ciṟupāṉmai amaikkappaṭum oru ciṟu vaḷaivu, kumpakkuṭam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭaṅkaṭkut taṭṭukaḷ eṉṟu peyar. kalliṟku allatu ōṭṭāñ callikku vaṭṭu eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam : akaṉṟa teruvum peru muṟṟamum poṭṭalum itai āṭiṭamām. itu ēṉai vakaikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟai : uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉ paṭiyō yār munti yāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇintukoṇṭu iruvaruḷ oruvar āṭat toṭaṅkuvar, tiruvuḷaccīṭṭut tīrppu, kācu cuṇṭi allatu vaṭṭeṟintu aṟiyappeṟum.
āṭupavar, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, iṭavaricai mutaṟkaṭṭat til tam vaṭṭaiyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil av vaṭṭai mitittu, ataik kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil atai mitittal vēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa vaṭṭu kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷ viḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil vaṭṭai mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum tūkkiya kālai āṭumpōtu kīḻē ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka iyalātavāṟu tolaiviṟ ceṉṟuviṭiṉum, āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam māṟi māṟik kaṭaipōka āṭuvar.
mutaṟkaṭṭa āṭṭut tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ, aṭutta iru kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, piṉpu camuttirattilum, ataṉpiṉ valappuṟa mukkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, muṟaiyē muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. 2ām, 3ām, 5ām, 6ām kaṭṭaṅkaḷil āṭumpōtu, kaṭṭantoṟum mitittu noṇṭiyaṭippatum, oru kaṭṭam iṭaiyiṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭippatumāka, iruvēṟu muṟaiyuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē perumpāṉmai. vaṭṭaik kālāl taḷḷuvatilum, mitikkak kūṭiya kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāyt taḷḷuvatum, orēyaṭiyāy veḷiyē taḷḷuvatum, eṉa irumuṟai yuṇṭu.
eṉṟum iṭavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavē araṅkiṟkuṭ pukavēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, iṭavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum camuttiramum muṟaiyē kīḻiruntu mēlākavum, valavaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē mēliruntu kīḻākavum, āṭappeṟum eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
iruvaricaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, araṅkiṉ muṉniṉṟu, āyiṉ araṅkup pakkam puṟaṅkāṭṭit talaikku mēlāka, camuttirattil viḻumāṟu vaṭṭaiyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittuk kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṭaiyil oṉṟum, iṟutiyil oṉṟumāka, irutaṭavai camuttirat til vaṭṭeṟintu āṭappaṭum. mutal taṭavai etirnōkkiyum, iraṇṭām taṭavai puṟaṅkāṭṭiyum, vaṭṭeṟivatē vēṟupāṭām.
yārēṉum oruvar iraṇṭāntaṭavai camuttirattil tavaṟātu āṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭai muṭiyum. veṟṟipeṟṟavar, araṅkiṟku veḷiyē muṉpuṟa mūlaiyoṉṟai yaṭuttu, kutiṅkālai naṭuvāka vūṉṟip peruviral allatu pātattāl oru vaṭṭam varaivar. ataṟku uppu eṉṟu peyar. atai varaital uppu vaittal eṉappaṭum. ovvoru veṟṟikkum ovvōr uppu muṉṉēr varicaiyāka vaikkappeṟum. munti veṉṟavar araṅkiṉ iṭappuṟa mūlaiyaṭuttum, pinti veṉṟavar ataṉ valappuṟa mūlai yaṭuttum uppuvaittal marapu. araṅkiṟku muṉṉāl taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu, araṅkiṉ mutaṟkaṭṭattiliruntu orē yeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu eṭṭattiliruppavaṉ, āṭakar tām vaitta uppu vaḻiyāka naṭantēṉum noṇṭiyaṭittēṉum ceṉṟu mitikkalām. itu uppu vaittavarukkum ēṟpaṭum vacati. iv vacati uppu mikutikkut takkavāṟu mikum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu, oru noṇṭi vaṇikaṉ palanāṭu kaṭantu arumporuḷ tēṭi vantataik kuṟittatāka irukkalām. 
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : oṟṟaikkāṟ celavu, kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oru poruḷai eṟital, kālāl oru poruḷaik kuṟitta iṭattiṟkut taḷḷutal. piṉpuṟamākak kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oṉṟai yeṟital mutaliya payiṟcikaḷ iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu muṟai
I. oṟṟaic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟ pāṇṭiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum vaḷaiyāṭṭu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. oṟṟaikkaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli oṟṟaic cilli eṉappaṭum.
cillikkup pāṇṭi eṉṉum peyarum cilaviṭattu aruki vaḻaṅkum.
 āṭuvār tokai : iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : nāṉku camakaṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatum, ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatumāṉa, oru nīḷ catura araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilli (cillākku) eṉappaṭum vaṭṭum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. araṅkiṉ mēṟpakutiyāṉa araivaṭṭat tiṟkuc cōḻanāṭṭil malai eṉṟum koṅkunāṭṭil karakam eṉṟum peyar. cēlam vaṭṭārattiṟ karakaṅ kīṟāmalum āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟai : mutalāvatu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu ak kaṭṭattait tāṇṭi aṭutta kaṭṭaṅkaḷai ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuc ceṉṟu karakattil allatu kaṭaicik kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē kālūṉṟi, mīṇṭum muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuk kīḻ vantu cilliyai mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, atai ōr eṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam piṟa kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarntu cilliyeṟintu āṭal vēṇṭum.
mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷil eṟinta cilliyai ovvoru kaṭṭamāyk kīḻē taḷḷikkoṇṭu vantu, aṭik kaṭṭattiliruntu veḷiyē taḷḷi orē yeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉūṭu cellumpōtum mīḷumpōtum, noṇṭiyaṭikka vēṇṭiyapōtellām, noṇṭiyaṭittē ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa cilli kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum, kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷ vīḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil cilliyai mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭikkumpōtu tūkkiya kālaik kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orēyeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu neṭuntolaiviṟkuc celliṉum āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭalvēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, āṭṭai muṭiyumvarai iruvarum māṟimāṟi yāṭuvar.
nālu kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintāṭiyapiṉ, akaṅkaiyil orumuṟaiyum puṟaṅkaiyil orumuṟaiyum kuttukkaimēl orumuṟai yum cilliyai vaittukkoṇṭu, karakamvarai noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. karakattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, talaimēl orumuṟaiyum valappātattiṉmēl orumuṟaiyum cilliyai vaittukkoṇṭu, ovvoru kaṭṭattai yum ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuk karakam varai naṭantu ceṉṟu naṭantu mīḷavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu atai mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu aṅku niṉṟu araṅkiṟku muṉpākac cilliyeṟintu, aṅkiruntu noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu atai mitittal vēṇṭum.
piṉṉar, mīṇṭum orumuṟai karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, kaṇṇai mūṭikkoṇṭāvatu mēṉōkkik koṇṭāvatu ovvoru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum ovvōr eṭṭuvaittu naṭantuceṉṟu, cilliyai mitittal vēṇṭum. ovvōr eṭṭuvaikkum pōtum amarēcā eṉṟu collavēṇṭum. kāl kōṭṭil paṭātu eṭṭuc cariyāyirup piṉ, etiriyār uṭaṉ uṭaṉ rēcu (amarēcu) eṉṟu vaḻimoḻi var; cariyāyillāviṭiṉ illai eṉpar. ataṉpiṉ etiriyār āṭalvēṇṭum.
amarēcāp pakuti tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ; etiriyārai yāṉaiyā, pūṉaiyā? eṉṟu viṉavi, yāṉai eṉṟu colliṉ nimirntu niṉṟu talaikku mēlākavum, pūṉai eṉṟu colliṉ kuṉintu irukāṟ kavaṭṭūṭum cilliyai oru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ eṟiyavēṇṭum. atu ek kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻukiṟatō akkaṭṭattil, mūlaik kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟalvēṇṭum. atu paḻam eṉappaṭum. paḻam pōṭṭa kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ etiriyār kāl vaittal kūṭātu; eṉṟum atait  tāṇṭiyē cella vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, etiriyāriṉ vacatikkāka, ak kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē valappuṟattil allatu iṭappuṟattil, kaṭṭattai yoṭṭi ōr araivaṭṭam amaittuk koṭukkappaṭum . ataṟku `yāṉaikkāl eṉṟu peyar. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr āṭakarum, tattam paḻakkaṭṭattiṉ pakkamāka etiriyārkku yāṉaikkāl amaittuk koṭuppar.
orumuṟai paḻam pōṭṭavar maṟumuṟai muntiyāṭuvar. maṟumuṟai paḻattiṟkuc cilliyeṟiyumpōtu, atu paḻak kaṭṭattil viḻuntuviṭiṉ tavaṟām.
oruvarēyō iruvarumō ellāk kaṭṭattilum paḻam pōṭṭapiṉ, araṅku kalaikkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum kīṟappaṭum.
II. iraṭṭaic cilli
mutalām vakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : iraṭṭaik kaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli iraṭṭaic cilliyām.
āṭuvār tokai : iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : varicaikku nāṉkāka iruvaricaikku eṭṭuc caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟōṭu malaiyuṅkoṇṭa, ōr araṅkum; āḷukkoru cilliyum; itai āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟai : itu perumpālum pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟaippaṭi āṭappeṟum.
iraṇṭām vakai
āṭumuṟai : itu ēṟattāḻa oṟṟaic cilli pōṉṟē āṭappeṟum. oṟṟaic cilliyil, ovvoru kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintapiṉ, karakam allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷivarai noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. itilō, valappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu iṭappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cilliyai mitittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. valappuṟa uccik kaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām. itaik kuṟittaṟku atil mūlaik kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟappaṭṭirukkum.
naṭantu cellavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷil, orē camayattil iru kālaiyum irupuṟak kaṭṭattilum vaittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
mūṉṟām vakai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : itu carikaip pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum. 
āṭumuṟai : ituvum oṟṟaic cilli pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ iruvaricaik kaṭṭaṅ kaḷilum āṭappeṟum. iṭavaricaiyil cilli yeṟiyumpōtu valavaricai vaḻiyākavum, valavaricaiyiṟ cilli yeṟiyumpōtu iṭa varicai vaḻiyākavum cellavēṇṭum. iṭa varicai yuccikkaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷa lām.
naṭantu cellavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷ mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām vakaiyaippōl āṭappeṟum.
III. vāṉūrtic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : vāṉūrtipōl araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli vāṉūrtic cilliyām. itai ērōppiḷēṉ (Aeroplane) cilli eṉa āṅkilac coṟkoṇṭē aḻaippar.
mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūrti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē inta āṭṭu āṭappaṭṭiruppiṉ, aṉṟu itaṟku vēṟoru tamiḻp peyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. 
āṭuvār tokai : iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭapaṭi ōr araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilliyum, itai āṭukaruviyākum.
āṭumuṟai : mutalāvatu, mutaṟ kaṭṭattil cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittu ak kaṭṭattait tāṇṭi, mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ  oṟṟaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām ovvōr eṭṭuvaittu noṇṭiyaṭittum, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭattiṟkoṉṟāka orēcamayattil irukālum ūṉṟiyum, mēliraṭṭaik kaṭṭam varai ceṉṟu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭuvantu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyai mitittu muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyētaḷḷi, atai orē eṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittal vēṇṭum.
āṉāl, kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu kutittu mēliraṭṭaik kaṭṭattiṟ kālūṉṟumpōtu, malainōkki ūṉṟāmal, ataṟkup puṟaṅkāṭṭik kutittu muṉpuṟam nōkki ūṉṟavēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam eṭṭāṅ kaṭṭamvarai āṭal vēṇṭum. cilli yirukkiṟa kaṭṭam etuvāyiṉum, mēlē cellumpōtu atai mitittal kūṭā tu; kīḻē varumpōtutāṉ atai mitittal vēṇṭum. ākavē kīḻiraṭṭaiyilāyiṉum mēliraṭṭaiyilāyiṉum cilli irukkumpōtu, atiṟ piṟacamayampōl irukālum ūṉṟa muṭiyātu. cilliyuḷḷa kaṭṭattai viṭṭuviṭṭuc cilliyillāta kaṭṭattait tāṉ oṟṟaik kaṭṭampōl noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. mēṟcellum pōtum kīḻvarumpōtum, kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇmuṟaippaṭiyē ceṉṟu varavēṇṭum. kaṭṭatti luḷḷa cilliyai mitittu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷumpōtu, kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamākavum taḷḷalām; orēyaṭiyākavun taḷḷalām.
eṭṭuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷum iṅṅaṉam āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, oṟṟaic cilliyiṟpōl, veḷḷaikkaiyum karuppukkaiyum kuttukkaiyum talaiyum kālum, muṟaiyē āṭappeṟum. talaiyuṅkālum āṭumpōtu, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil irukālum vaittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, malaikkuc cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷi avvāṟē vantu mitittal vēṇṭum. ip pakuti noṇṭi eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum. itilum, iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil irukālum ūṉṟavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, malaikku mīṇṭu cilli yeṟintu amarēcā āṭicceṉṟu mitittal vēṇṭum. iraṭṭaik kaṭṭattil orē camaiyattil irukāl vaittal vēṇṭum.
amarēcā muṭintapiṉ, malaiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu  etiriyārai yāṉaiyā, pūṉaiyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu,            patilukkēṟpac cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittuvantu atai       mitittu, cilli viḻunta kaṭṭattil oru kālāl niṉṟukoṇṭu, paḻam pōṭṭuviṭṭuc cellavēṇṭumā? vantu paḻam pōṭa vēṇṭumā? eṉṟu etiriyārai viṉavi, muṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ pōṭṭuviṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭittu muṉvaravēṇṭum; piṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittu araṅkiṟku muṉpuṟamāka vantapiṉ ceṉṟu pōṭavēṇṭum; pōṭṭapiṉ āṭṭai muṭiyum.
noṇṭiyilum amarēcāvilum tavira, piṟa pakuti
kaḷil malaikkuc celvatillai.
āṭṭattil tavaṟum vakaiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻum ceyalum, piṟa cillikaṭkuk kūṟiyavaiyē.
ōr āṭṭaiyil veṉṟavar aṭutta āṭṭaiyil muntiyāṭuvar.
IV. vaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : vaṭṭamāṉa araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli vaṭṭac cilliyām.
āṭukaruvi : cakkaravaṭimāṉa ōr araṅkum āḷukkoru cilliyum itai āṭu karuviyām.
cakkarattiṉ kuṟaṭṭil oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭat tokaiyum, ataṉ āraikaṭkiṭaiyil muṟaiyē at tokaikku varicai yoḻuṅkākak kīḻppaṭṭa ciṟutokaikaḷum, kuṟikkappaṭum.
āṭumuṟai : mutalāvatu kīḻttokaiyuḷḷa kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu atai orēyeṭṭil mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum orēyeṭṭil atai mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu mēṉmēluyarnta tokaiyuḷḷa cuṟṟuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām āṭiyapiṉ, naṭukkaṭṭattil āṭal vēṇṭum. naṭukkaṭṭattuḷ eṟinta cilliyai, atu aṅkirukkumpōtum atai veḷiyē taḷḷiya piṉpum, nērē orēyeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟic cuṟṟuk kaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyāyc ceṉṟu mitittal kūṭātu.
oruvar tavaṟiyapiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum.
kuṟitta vaṭṭat tokaiyai munti yeṭuttavar (atāvatu ellāk kaṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum tavaṟātu muntiyāṭiyavar), kelittava rāvar. kelippataṟkup paḻam eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru paḻattiṟkum kālāl ovvōr uppu vaikkappaṭum.  uppukkuḷ, vaṭṭaiyi ṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭṭukkoṇṭu varicaiyāka irukkum. veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli oreyeṭ ṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu tolaiviliruppiṉ, āṭupavar tām vaitta uppu varicaimēl naṭantuceṉṟu atai mitikkalām.
V. kālippaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kālippaṭṭam pōl araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli kālip paṭṭac cilliyām.
kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkaviṭum paṭṭattaik kālippaṭṭam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattār.
āṭukaruvi : mūlaikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa oru caturamum, ataiyoṭṭiya paṭukkaiyāṉa oru ciṟu nīḷ caturamum koṇṭa oru peru nīḷcatura araṅkum; āḷukkoru cilliyum; itai āṭu karuviyām. mūlaik kuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa caturam nāṉku kaṭṭamāka amaiyum. mēluḷḷa nīḷcaturam aintāṅ kaṭṭamākum.
āṭumuṟai : mutalāvatu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, atai orēyeṭṭil noṇ ṭiyaṭittu mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum atai muṉpōl mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cilli yeṟintu, eṇ muṟaippaṭi kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāy noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, nēraṭiyākavō kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavō veḷiyē taḷḷi, muṉ pōṉṟē noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu mitittal vēṇṭum.
malai vēṇṭuvāruḷ cilar, aintāṅ kaṭṭattai malaiyākak koḷvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātār, ataṉ mēṟpuṟa veḷiyai aṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭuttuvar. malaikkuc cilli yeṟiyumpōtu etirnōkkiyum, malaiyiliruntu cilli yeṟiyumpōtu puṟaṅkāṭṭit talaikku mēlākavum, eṟivatu vaḻakkam. paḻamāṉavar uppu vaippar. iṅṅaṉam eḷiyamuṟaiyil itai āṭuvatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkamām.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil, mēṟkūṟiya pakutikaḷōṭu veḷḷaikkai karuppukkai kuttukkai talai kāl amarēcā eṉpavaṟṟaiyum cērttukkoḷvar.
iṉi, uttiyil niṉṟu cilliyeṟintu, 4ām allatu 5ām kaṭṭattil vīḻiṉ uṭaṉē paḻamāvatum, piṟakaṭṭaṅkaḷil vīḻiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, uttivarai noṇṭiyaṭittut taḷḷikkoṇṭupōyp paḻamāvatum; cēlam vaṭṭārattil maṟṟoru vakaiyāy itai āṭum muṟaiyām.
VI. kaiccilli
taṉiyāyirukkum ciṟuvaṉ allatu ciṟumi, kīḻē uṭkārntu oṟṟaic cilliyaraṅku ciṟiyatāy varaintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralaik kālpōṟ pāvittuk kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy vaittuc ceṉṟu, āṭik koḷḷum āṭṭu kaiccilliyām. itu cēlam vaṭṭārattil āṭappeṟum.
3. kampa viḷaiyāṭṭu
nālvar, oru maṇṭapattiṉ allatu catura iṭattiṉ nāṉku mūlaik kampattilum, kampattiṟkoruvarāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, etirum valamum allatu etirum iṭamumāka, māṟi māṟi allatu cuṟṟiccuṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu kampattiṟku iyaṅkikkoṇṭirukka, iṉṉoruvar avarait toṭalvēṇṭum. muṉṉarē ētēṉu moruvakaiyil tōṟṟavar allatu tavaṟiyavar toṭavēṇ ṭiyavarāvar.
oruvar kampattaiviṭṭu vilakiyirukkumpōtum, ēṟkeṉavē āḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru kampattaic cērntirukkum pōtum, avarait toṭalām. toṭappaṭṭavar toṭṭavar viṉaiyaiyum, toṭṭavar toṭappaṭṭavar viṉaiyaiyum, mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
toṭukiṟavar, pāl mōr tayir viṟpavarpōl, pālō 
pāl! allatu mōrō mōr! allatu tayirō tayir! eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu tirivatu, cōḻa koṅkunāṭṭu vaḻakkum, uccantalaiyaik kaiyāl taṭṭikkoṇṭu toṇṭāṉ 
toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟu collit tirivatu, pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakkum ākum. pāṇṭināṭṭil iv viḷaiyāṭ ṭiṟkut toṇṭāṉ toṇṭāṉ toṭupiṭi toṇṭāṉ eṉṟē  peyar.
tīṭṭuḷḷa allatu tīṇṭappaṭāta oruvar tammiṉiṉṟu vilakiyōṭiya piṟarai, viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkō kuṟumpiṟkō toṭṭa ceyalai, naṭittuk kāṭṭuvatākavuḷḷatu iv viḷaiyāṭṭu.
4. kaccakkāyc cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kaccakkāyaic cilliyāl aṭittu āṭum āṭṭuk kaccakkāyc cilli.
āṭuvār tokai : palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : āḷukkup pattiṟkuk kuṟaiyāta pala kaccakkāy kaḷum, akaṉṟa cilliyum, ōraṭi viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamum, itai āṭukaruviyām. cillikku vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭattil cappātti eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalil itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟai : āṭakar nāṟkacat tolaiviluḷḷa uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ tattam cilliyai eṟivar. yāruṭaiyatu vaṭṭattiṟku allatu vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviṟku mika neruṅki yirukkiṉṟatō, avar muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. ēṉaiyōrellām tattam aṇmai muṟaippaṭi muṉ piṉṉāka āṭuvar.
āṭakarellārum tattam kaccakkāykaḷai vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ iṭṭapiṉ, ovvoruvarum uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ parappi allatu kuvittu vaikkappaṭṭirukkum kaccakkāykaḷait tattam cilliyāl aṭittalvēṇṭum. vaṭṭattiṟku veḷicceṉṟa kāykaḷaiyellām āṭuvōr eṭuttukkoḷḷalām.
cilli vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ cellāviṭiṉum, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ceṉṟum kāykaḷai aṭittu veḷiyēṟṟāviṭiṉum, kāykaḷai veḷiyēṟṟiyakkālum cilliyum uṭaṉ veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉum, tavaṟām. tavaṟiviṭiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum; tavaṟāviṭiṉ toṭarntu āṭalām. iṟutiyil, mikutiyāṉa kāykaḷai vaittiruppavar kelittavarāvar.
cēlam vaṭṭārattil kaccakkāykkup patilākac ciṟu curuṭṭup peṭṭiyaiyum vaittu āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu cikareṭ pākuc cilli eṉappaṭum.
5. kuñcu
āṭuvār iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, oru kaṭciyār pañcāram allatu āṭṭuk kūṇṭaḷavuḷḷa oru vaṭṭakkōṭṭiṉ arukum, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār caṟṟut tolaivilum niṟpar. tolaivil niṟpavar, tammuḷ oruvaraik kuñcu eṉa viḷampi, etirk kaṭciyil tattam uttiyaip piṭikkac celvar. etirk kaṭciyār kuñciṉaip piṭikka muyalvar. kuñcu kūṭuvantu cērntuviṭiṉ (atāvatu piṭipaṭātu vaṭṭak kōṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuntuviṭiṉ), atē kaṭciyār mīṇṭum kuñcuvaittu āṭuvar; piṭipaṭṭuviṭiṉ, kūṭṭiṉaruku niṉṟavar kuñcuvaittu āṭal vēṇṭum.
kōḻikkuñcu paruntiṟkut tappik kūṭṭiṟkuḷ allatu vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuvatai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭuk kuṟippatupōlum!
(2) iravāṭṭu
1. kaṇṇāmpotti
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar oru piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottik koṇṭirukkumpōtu, piṟa piḷḷaikaḷ ōṭi oḷiyum viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaṇṇāmpotti eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟai : mutiyār oruvar, āṭa virumpum piḷḷaikaḷai yellām oruṅkē iruttikkoṇṭu, ovvoruvaraiyum cuṭṭi oru maraput toṭaraic colli, at toṭariṉ iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottuvar. iṉi, mutiyār oruvarmītu ellāp piḷḷaikaḷum paṭapaṭaveṉṟu kaiyālaṭikkumpōtu, am mutiyāriṉ kaiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottuvatumuṇṭu. pottum pōtu, maṟṟap piḷḷaikaḷellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvar.
mutiyār akappaṭṭukkoṇṭa piḷḷaiyiṉ kaṇṇaip pottikkoṇṭirukkumpōtu, av viruvarukkum piṉvarumāṟu uraiyāṭṭu nikaḻum.
mutiyavar :	kaṇṇām pottiyārē kaṇṇām pottiyārē! 1
piḷḷai :	eṉṉa?
mu :	ettaṉai muṭṭaiyiṭṭāy? 2
pi :	mūṉṟu muṭṭaiyiṭṭēṉ.
1.	`kaṇṇām pottiyārē eṉpatu koccai vaṭivil `kaṇṇāmpūcciyārē eṉṟu tiriyum.
2.	viḷi uyarvup paṉmaiyiliruppiṉum, payaṉilai orumaiyākavē yirukkum. 
itu vaḻuvē.
mu :	avaṟṟuḷ oru muṭṭaiyaip porittut tiṉṟu viṭṭu, oru muṭṭaiyaip puḷitta taṇṇīrukkuḷ pōṭṭuviṭṭu, oru muṭṭaiyaip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.
pulā luṇṇātārāyiṉ, iv vuraiyāṭṭil viḷikkup piṟpaṭṭa viṉā, viṭai, ēval piṉvarumāṟirukkum.
mu :	ettaṉai paḻam paṟittāy?
pi :	mūṉṟu paḻam paṟittēṉ
mu :	avaṟṟuḷ oru paḻattaip piḷḷaiyārukkup paṭaittuviṭṭu, oru paḻattai aṟuttut tiṉṟu viṭṭu, oru paḻattaip piṭittuk koṇṭuvā.
iṉi, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu uraiyāṭṭiṉṟi,
kaṇṇāṅ kaṇṇām pūcci!
kāṭṭuttalai mūcci
ūḷai muṭṭaiyait tiṉṟuviṭṭu
nalla muṭṭai koṇṭuvā
eṉṟu ēvuṅ koṅkunāṭṭu vaḻakkum uḷatu.
iṅṅaṉam ēvappaṭṭa piḷḷai, uṭaṉē ōṭippōy aṅkumiṅkum pārttu oḷintukoṇṭirukkum piḷḷaikaḷuḷ oruvarait toṭamuyalum. mutalāvatu toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷai aṭuttamuṟai kaṇ pottappaṭum. toṭappaṭumuṉ ōṭivantu mutiyārait toṭṭuviṭṭāl, piṉpu toṭutal kūṭātu. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu kaṇpottiya piḷḷaiyē maṟumuṟaiyuṅ kaṇpottappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : paḷḷikkuc cellātu oḷintu tiriyum tiṇṇaippaḷḷi māṇavaraic caṭṭanampip piḷḷai piṭittu varuvatiṉiṉṟō, ciṟaikkut tappi oḷintu tiriyum kuṟṟavāḷi kaḷai ūrkāvalar piṭittuvaruvatiṉiṉṟō, iv viḷaiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
2. pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai yuruṭṭal
itu oruvāṟu kaṇṇāmpotti pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ itaṟku oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum; atōṭu muntit toṭuvatu yār eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṟku, ellāraiyum varicaiyāyk kuṉiya vaittu, avarkaḷ niḻalil pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyai oruvar 
uruṭṭuvar. atu yār niḻaliṟpōy niṟkiṉṟatō, avar ēṉaiyōrait toṭutal vēṇṭum.
piṟarellām ōṭi oḷintukoḷḷutaṟku iraṇṭoru nimaiyaṅ koṭukkappaṭum. toṭum piḷḷai toṭavarumpōtu, ellārum ōṭippōy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟukoḷvar. vaṭṭattiṟ kuṭ ceṉṟapiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ cellumuṉ toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷai aṭutta muṟai toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu toṭamuyaṉṟa piḷḷaiyē mīṇṭum toṭutal vēṇṭum.
oruvar toṭarntu mūvāṭṭai oruvaraiyun toṭāviṭiṉ, avarmītu ēṉaiyōr ciṟitu ciṟitu kutiraiyēṟuvatuṇṭu. ataṉpiṉ, mīṇṭum pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai uruṭṭappaṭum.
3. pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai yeṭuttal
ituvum oḷintu viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭṭē.
āṭuvārellām uttikaṭṭi irukaṭciyākap pirintu koṇṭapiṉ, oru kaṭciyār ōṭi oḷintukoḷvar. iṉṉoru kaṭciyār, kaṭaikaṭkuc ceṉṟu pukaiyilaik kaṭṭai (allatu veṟṟilaikkāmpu) eṭuttuvantu, oḷintavarait tēṭippiṭippar. yāraiyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭittapōtu, kaṭaikkuc ceṉṟu vantamaikku aṭaiyāḷamākap pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyaik kāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar muṉpu ellārum niṉṟa iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. ellāruṅ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭapiṉ viṉaimāṟi viḷaiyāṭuvar.
oḷintiruntavaraik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtu pukaiyilaik kaṭṭaiyaik kāṭṭāviṭiṉum, yāraiyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉum, muṉpu kaṇṭupiṭittavarē mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu pāṇṭināṭṭu viḷaiyāṭṭu. itaṉ cōḻanāṭṭu vakai varumāṟu :
āṭuvārellārum kaipōṭṭu ovvoruvarākap         pirittu, iṟutiyilakappaṭṭuk koṇṭavar ēṉaiyāraip piṭittalvēṇṭum. piṭikkavēṇṭiyavar caṟṟut tolaivi         luḷḷa oru kuṟippiṭṭa taḻaiyaik koṇṭuvanta piṉputāṉ           piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṟkuḷ ēṉaiyarellām maṟaivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil oḷintukoḷvar. kaṇṭupiṭippavar oḷintiruppa         varaik kaiyiṉāṟ piṭittukkoḷḷalām, allatu avar peyaraimaṭṭum piṟarkkuk kēṭkumāṟu urakkac collalām. iv viraṇṭil etu ceyvateṉṟu muṉṉarē tīrmāṉikkap         paṭṭirukkum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar taḻaiyaik kāṭṭac collumpōtu, kaṇṭupiṭittavar kāṭṭal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ, maṟumuṟaiyum avarē kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. taḻai kāṭṭappaṭiṉ, kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar maṟumuṟai kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. iv viḷaiyāṭṭu kulīmtār eṉṉum urutup peyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itait taṉittamiḻil taḻaipaṟittal eṉalām.
4. pūcci
pūcci eṉpatu āḷniḻal. nilavoḷiyiṟ pūcci teriyum pōtu oruvarait toṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu, pūcci allatu pūcci viḷaiyāṭṭu. āṭuvāruḷ ētēṉumoru vakaiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa oruvar, nilavoḷiyiṭattil niṟka, ēṉaiyarellām arukēyuḷḷa ōr iruṇṭa iṭattil niṉṟukoḷvar. iruṇṭa iṭattil niṟpavar oḷiyiṭattiṟku variṉ, avarait toṭalām; illāviṭiṉ toṭal kūṭātu. oḷiyiṭattil toṭappaṭṭavar piṉpu piṟarait toṭutal vēṇṭum.
5. aracaṉun tōṭṭamum
aracaṉum cēvakaṉum tōṭṭakkāraṉum tōṭṭamum (atāvatu pūcaṇikkoṭikaḷum) eṉa āṭuvār mutalāvatu pirintukoḷvar.
aracaṉ caṟṟu eṭṭattiliruntu koṇṭu, pūcaṇit tōṭṭam pōṭumpaṭi cēvakaṉvaḻit tōṭṭakkāraṉai ēvuvaṉ. tōṭṭak kāraṉ vēlai toṭaṅkuvaṉ.
aracaṉ aṭikkaṭi taṉ cēvakaṉai ēvit tōṭṭattiṉ nilaimaiyaip pārttuviṭṭu varac colvaṉ. cēvakaṉ vantu kēṭkum ovvoru taṭavaiyum, muṟaiyē, ovvoru payirttoḻil viṉai nikaḻntuḷḷatākat tōṭṭakkāraṉ colvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam uḻutal, vitaittal, nīr pāyccal. muḷaittal, ōrilai mutaṟ pala ilaivarai viṭal, koṭiyōṭal, kaḷaiyeṭuttal, pūppūttal, piñcuviṭal, kāy ātal, muṟṟutal ākiya pala viṉaikaḷum collappaṭum. uḻutal mutaṟ kāy ātal varai ovvoru viṉaiyaic collumpōtum, tōṭṭattai nikarkkum piḷḷaikaḷ avvav viṉaiyaik kaiyāl naṭittuk kāṭṭuvar.
kāykaḷ muṟṟiyataic collumuṉ, tōṭṭakkāraṉ         kāykaḷait taṭṭippārppatupōl ovvoru piḷḷaiyiṉ talaiyaiyum kuṭṭi muṟṟiviṭṭatā? eṉṟu taṉṉaittāṉē kēṭṭu, cila talaikaḷai muṟṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, cila talaikaḷai muṟṟavillai eṉṟum, colli; muṟṟiviṭṭateṉṟu coṉṉa piḷḷaikaḷai vēṟāka vaippāṉ. kāy koṇṭuvarumpaṭi aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa cēvakaṉ cila kāykaḷaik koṇṭupōyt taṉ vīṭṭil vaittiruppatupōl, cila piḷḷaikaḷaik koṇṭu pōyc caṟṟut tolaivil vaittiruppāṉ. ak kāykaḷ kaḷavu pōvatupōl, ap piḷḷaikaḷ tām muṉpirunta iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭorumuṟai nikaḻntapiṉ, cēvakaṉ aracaṉaiyē aḻaittu vantu, ellāk kāykaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭu pōvatupōl ellāp piḷḷaikaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭupōvaṉ. itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum. iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭai.
6. kulaikulaiyāy muntirikkāy
pala piḷḷaikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uḷnōkki uṭkārntirukka, oru piḷḷai tiripōl muṟukkiya tuṇiyoṉṟaik kaiyil vaittukkoṇṭu, kulaikulaiyāy muntirikkāy eṉṟu viṭṭu viṭṭu urakkac colli, vaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē piḷḷai kaṭku arukil valamākac cuṟṟic cuṟṟivarum. ap piḷḷai kulai.... kāy eṉṟu colluntoṟum, uṭkārntirukkum piḷḷaika ḷellām oruṅkē nariyē, nariyē, ōṭivā eṉṟu kattic colvar.
iṅṅaṉam nikaḻntu koṇṭirukkumpōtē, cuṟṟi varum piḷḷai tiṭumeṉṟu tiriyai oru piḷḷaiyiṉ piṉṉāl vaittuviṭum; tirivaikkappaṭṭa piḷḷai uṭaṉē kaṇṭu atai eṭukkāviṭiṉ, vaittapiḷḷai oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu, vaikkap paṭṭa piḷḷaiyiṉ mutukil at tiriyāl ōraṭi ōṅkivaittu eḻuppi, atai ap piḷḷaiyiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, ap piḷḷai yiṉ iṭattil tāṉ uṭkārntukoḷḷum. tiri vaittavuṭaṉ vaikkappaṭṭa piḷḷai kaṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭu eḻuntuviṭiṉ, vaitta piḷḷai viraintu oru cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi vantu veṟṟiṭattil uṭkārntu aṭikkut  tappikkoḷḷum.
tiriyeṭutta allatu tirivāṅkiṉa piḷḷai, muṉpōl kulai... kāy, eṉṟu muṉcolla, maṟṟap piḷḷaikaḷellām, nariyē.. ...th” eṉṟu piṉ colvar. piṉpu tirivaippatumpiṟavu«nikaḻum. iṅṅaṉamtoṭarnJāṭappeṟum.
iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkut tirittiri pantam eṉṟu peyar.
pāṇṭināṭṭil, piḷḷaikaḷ vaṭṭamāy uṭkārātu varicaiyāy uṭkārntu, iv viḷaiyāṭṭai āṭuvatuṇṭu. atu yāṉaittiri eṉṟu peyar peṟum.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
1. noṇṭi
āṭiṭamum poḻutum āṭuvārum : noṇṭi eṉpatu, poṭṭalilum tiṟanta veḷinilattilum, perumpālum ciṟuvarum ciṟupāṉmai ciṟumiyarum, pakalilum nilaviravilum viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu.
