veḷiniṟkum kaṭciyārum aṉaivarum noṇṭiyaṭittu muṭintapiṉ, allatu uḷniṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ aṉaivarum toṭappaṭṭapiṉ, ōr āṭṭam muṭiyum.
āṭṭa veṟṟi : uḷniṟpār aṉaivarum toṭappaṭṭu viṭiṉ veḷiniṟpārkkum, aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi oruvar eñciyirup piṉum uḷ niṟpārkkum, veṟṟiyām.
āṭṭat toṭarcci : orumuṟai yāṭiyapiṉ, mīṇṭum orumuṟaiyō pala muṟaiyō āṭa nēramum viruppamumiruppiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceyvar. muntiya āṭṭattil veṉṟa kaṭciyārē pintiya āṭṭattilum uḷniṟpar. uḷniṟpatē iṉiyatākak karutappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷaiyāṭṭup pōrviṉai
yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum. pōrkkaḷattil oru kāl veṭṭuṇṭa maṟavaṉ maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟē, tāṉ paṭṭu vīḻuntuṇaiyum, pakaivarai veṭṭi vīḻttuvatuṇṭu. im maṟa viṉaiyai naṭittukkāṭṭum mukamākavē iv viḷaiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
kuṟaittalaikaḷ kūttāṭuvatum talaiyaik kaiyilēnti niṟpatum pōṉṟa maṟaviṉaikaḷai nōkkumpōtu, oru kāṟ kuṟaikaḷ noṇṭic ceṉṟu nūḻilāṭṭuvatu viyappaṉṟu.
kaḷvaṉoruvaṉ paṭaiyiluḷḷa kutiraiyoṉṟait tiruṭa muyaṉṟu kāl taṟiyuṇṭapiṉ nalvaḻippaṭṭa ceytiyaic cintuc ceyyuḷāṟ puṉaintu kūṟum noṇṭi nāṭakam eṉṉum nāṭaka nūlvakaiyum uḷatu. cītakkāti noṇṭi nāṭakam itaṟkōr  eṭuttukkāṭṭām. in nāṭakac ceyti mēṟkūṟiya pōr viṉaic ceytiyiṉ vēṟām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : oru kāl nōyppaṭṭum veṭṭuṇṭum naṭakkaviyalātapōtu maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittu vēṇṭu miṭañ celvataṟkāṉa payiṟciyai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu aḷikkum.
2. niṉṟāl piṭittukkoḷ
ētēṉum oru vakaiyil akappaṭṭukkoṇṭapiḷḷai piṟa piḷḷaikaḷai niṉṟāl toṭavēṇṭum; uṭkārntu koṇṭāl toṭakkūṭātu. toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷai, piṉpu piṟarait toṭal vēṇṭum.
3. paruppuc caṭṭi
pala piḷḷaikaḷ viṟaku viṟaku eṉṟu collik koṇṭu, vaṭṭamāyc cuṟṟi varavēṇṭum. oru periya piḷḷai avarkaḷai appaṭiyē uṭkārac colli, vaṭṭattuḷ niṉṟu uṅkaḷ vīṭṭil eṉṉa kuḻampu? eṉṟu varicaippaṭi ovvoruvaraiyum kēṭkum. ovvoruvarum paruppallāta ovvoru kuḻampaic colvar. piṉpu, iṟutiyil ellāp piḷḷaikaḷuñ cērntu, ap periya piḷḷaiyai avvāṟē kēṭpar. ap piḷḷai paruppukkuḻampu eṉṉum. uṭaṉē ellārum eḻuntiruntu, ap piḷḷaiyaip paruppuccaṭṭi eṉa aḻaittu nakaiyāṭi makiḻvar.
nāḷtōṟum paruppuk kuḻampaiyē virumpiyuṇṇum oruvaraip paḻippatupōl uḷḷatu,  iv viḷaiyāṭṭu.
4. mōtiram vaittal
āṭuvārellārum irukaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcikkoruvarāka iruvaroḻiya ēṉaiyarellām, kaṭci vāriyāy iru varicaiyāka etiretir uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārāta iruvarum tattam kaṭci varicaiyiṉ piṉṉāl niṉṟukoṇṭiruppar. avaruḷ oruvar oru mōtirattaik kaiyil vaittukkoṇṭu kuṉintu, tam varicaiyil ovvoruvar piṉṉālum atai vaippatāka naṭittu, yārēṉum oruvar piṉṉāl vaittuviṭṭu, varicai neṭukalum ceṉṟapiṉ nimirntu niṟpar. etir varicaikkup piṉṉāl niṟpavar, mōtiram vaikkappaṭṭa iṭattai iṉṉārukkup piṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭum. cariyāyc colliviṭiṉ, aṭutta muṟai etir varicaiyāḷ mōtiram vaittal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ muṉvaittavarē vaittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu āṭappeṟum. kīḻē yiruppavar āṭṭu neṭukalum uṭkārntu koṇṭē yiruppar.
5. puliyum āṭum
 pala piḷḷaikaḷ vaṭṭamāyk kaikōttu niṟka, oru piḷḷai uḷḷum maṟṟoru piḷḷai veḷiyum niṟpar. uḷ niṟkum piḷḷai āṭākavum, veḷi niṟkum piḷḷai puliyākavum, pāvikkap peṟuvar. pulikkum vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷaikaṭkum piṉ varumāṟu uraiyāṭṭu nikaḻum:
puli	:	caṅkili puṅkili katavait tiṟa.
piḷḷaikaḷ	:	nāṉmāṭṭēṉ vēṅkaippuli.
puli	:	varalāmā? varakkūṭātā?
piḷḷaikaḷ	:	varakkūṭātu.
piḷḷaikaḷ vaḻimaṟuttapiṉ, puli yārēṉum irupiḷḷaikaṭ kiṭaiyil nuḻaiyap pārkkum. piḷḷaikaḷ iṭam viṭuvatillai. palamuṟai aṅkumiṅkum cuṟṟip pārttapiṉ, puli tiṭumeṉṟu ōriṭattil valintu pukum. uṭaṉē āṭu veḷiyē viṭappeṟum. puli āṭṭaip piṭikka veḷiyēṟum. āṭu mīṇṭum uḷḷē viṭappeṟum. iṅṅaṉam māṟimāṟi iraṇṭorumuṟai nikaḻntu piṉ, iṟutiyil puli āṭṭaip piṭittukkoḷḷum.
iv viḷaiyāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭai. vaṭṭamāy niṟkum piḷḷaikaḷ āṭṭut toḻuvattai nikarppar.
6. ‘ïbj‹d _£il?
pala piḷḷaikaḷ kūṭiṉaviṭattu, oru periya piḷḷai piṟaraiyellām vaṭṭamāka irutti, avarkaḷ kaikaḷai virittu nilattiṉmēṟ kuppuṟa vaikkac ceytu, oru maraputtoṭarc coṟkaḷait taṉittaṉi colli ovvoru collālum ovvoru kaiyaic cuṭṭi, iṟutic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa piḷḷaiyai uṅkaḷ appaṉ pēr eṉṉa? eṉṟu kēṭkum. ataṟku ap piḷḷai muruṅkaippū eṉṉum. piṉpu ap periya piḷḷai muruṅkaippū tiṉṟavaṉē? (ḷē!) muḷḷān taṇṇīr kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē!) pāmpukkai paṭakkeṉṟu eṭuttuk koḷ eṉṟu collum. muruṅkaippū....... kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṉum pakutiyāl, collukkoruvarāka nāṉku piḷḷaikaḷ cuṭṭappeṟum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu muṭiyum piḷḷai, uṭaṉē oru kaiyai eṭuttup piṉṉāl vaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kuṭittavaṉē! (ḷē) eṉṟu iraṇṭām muṟai muṭiyum piḷḷai, iṉṉoru kaiyaiyum eṭuttup piṉṉāl vaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tirumpat tirumpac ceyyiṉ, iṟutiyil oru piḷḷai akappaṭṭukkoḷḷum.
ap piḷḷai taṉ irukaikaḷaiyum maṭakki oṉṟaṉmē loṉṟāyk kīḻēvaikka, maṟṟap piḷḷaikaḷum tam kaikaḷai avvāṟē avaṟṟiṉmēl aṭukki vaippar.
periya piḷḷai kīḻē cāṇipōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? maṇpōṭṭu meḻukalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, piṟar cāṇipōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, taṉ akaṅkaiyāl aṭikkaiyiṉ kīḻp pūcuvatupōl taṭavavēṇṭum; maṇpōṭṭu meḻuku eṉṟāl, puṟaṅkaiyāl avvāṟu ceyyavēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭum, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? tīṭṭāta kattiyil veṭṭalāmā? eṉṟu periya piḷḷai kēṭṭu, tīṭṭiṉa kattiyil eṉṟāl aintuviralum nerukki nīṭṭip pakkavāṭ          ṭālum, tīṭṭāta kattiyil eṉṟāl maṇikkaip pakkattālum veṭṭavēṇṭum.
 iṉi, veṭṭuñ ceyalpaṟṟi, muṟkūṟiya viṉāviṟkup patilāka, aṭiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? nuṉiyil veṭṭaṭṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, aṭiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl kuttukkaiyaṭukkiṉ aṭiyilum, nuṉiyil veṭṭu eṉṟāl ataṉ nuṉiyilum veṭṭuvatumuṇṭu.
piṉpu mīṇṭum muṉmuṟaippaṭi kaikaḷai aṭukki vaikkavēṇṭum. periya piḷḷai ovvoru kaiyāyt toṭṭu iteṉṉa mūṭṭai? iteṉṉa mūṭṭai? eṉṟu kēṭkum. piṟar arici mūṭṭai, paruppu mūṭṭai, puḷi mūṭṭai, uppu mūṭṭai eṉa ovvoru carakkiṉ peyarāṟ kūṟuvar. ovvoru mūṭṭaiyilum koñcaṅ koñcan tāruṅkaḷ eṉṟu periya piḷḷai kēṭkum. piṟar taramāṭṭōm eṉpar. ataṉāṟ periya piḷḷai, ellāraiyum kaṇṇai mūṭikkoṇṭu kuppuṟak kaviḻntukoḷḷac colvāḷ. avar aṅṅaṉañ ceytapiṉ, uṅkaḷ appā varukiṟārkaḷ, uṅkaḷ ammā varukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu ēmāṟṟi yāraiyēṉum eḻavaittu, ap piḷḷaikku oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokaiyāṉa aṭi periyapiḷḷai koṭukkum. oru varum ēmāṟi eḻāviṭiṉ, viḷaiyāṭṭup pōtum, eḻuntiruṅkaḷ eṉṟu colli, ellārum eḻuntapiṉ ellārkkum aṭikoṭukkum.  itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
7. kummi
pala pētaiyarum1 petumpaiyarum, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivantu pāṭik kaikuvittu aṭikkum kūttu, kummi eṉappaṭum. vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār itaik koppi eṉpar.
kummutal kaikuvittal allatu kaikuvit taṭittal. kaikuvit taṭikkum viḷaiyāṭṭātalāl, itu kummiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
kummi yāṭṭattiṟkeṉṟu taṉivakaip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu kummiyaṭi eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatōṭu, at toṭaraiyē ovvōr uruvilum (caraṇattilum) makuṭamākavuṅ koṇ ṭirukkum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu
kummiyaṭi peṇṇē kummiyaṭi - nalla
koṉṟai malarcūṭik kummiyaṭi
 nammaiyā ḷumtaṉi nāyakam nammiṭam
naṇṇiya teṉṟunī kummiyaṭi
 āṭcimoḻi yiṅkē āṅkilamāy - eṉṟum
āki viṭiṉatu kēṭākum
 māṭci mikuntamiḻ mānilat tāḷukai
mātara cēvarak kummiyaṭi.
ikkālai, oṟṟait tāḷattiṟkum aṭittāḷattiṟkum 
ēṟkum ellāp pāṭṭukaḷum kummikkum pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṟṟai = ēkam. aṭi = āti.
1.	aintāṇṭu mutal ēḻāṇṭuvaraippaṭṭa peṇ pētaiyeṉappaṭuvāḷ.
2
kuḻantaip pakkam
naṟṟāyarum cevilittāyarum piṟarum, aiyāṇṭiṟkuk kīḻppaṭṭa kuḻantaikaḷaik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭattokuti, kuḻantaip pakkam ākum.
irupoḻutāṭṭu
1 . cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē
mutiyār oruvar oru kuḻantaiyiṉ kaiyai akaṅkai mēṉōkkap piṭittukkoṇṭu, ataṉ aintu viralkaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟāka muṟaiyē toṭṭu itu aiyāviṟku, itu ammāviṟku, itu aṇṇaṉukku, itu akkāviṟku, itu uṉakku, eṉṟu kūṟi, av vakaṅkaiyiṟ paruppuk kaṭaivatupōl tam kaiyāl naṭittukkāṭṭi, piṉpu mīṇṭum paruppuk kaṭaintu, cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉṟu collik koṇṭu tam kaiyai ak kuḻantaiyiṉ akkuḷvarai mella iḻuttucceṉṟu, kiccuk kiccu eṉṟu collik kiccaṅ kāṭṭuvar. itu kuḻantaikku makiḻcciyum cirippaiyum uṇṭu paṇṇum.
paruppuk kaṭaivatupōṟ ceyyumpōtu, paruppuk kaṭaintu paruppuk kaṭaintu eṉṟum, paruppaip pakirvatupōṟ ceyyum pōtu, intā uṉakku, intā uṉakku eṉṟum collappaṭum.
vaḻakkamāyk kūḻuṅ kañciyum uṇpavar, cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpatiṟkup patilāka, kañci koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpar. ataṉāl avar at  toṭa rālēyē iv viḷaiyāṭṭaik kuṟippar.
tolaivāṉa iṭattil vēlai ceyyum aṇṇaṉukkup paruppum cōṟum koṇṭupōkum taṅkaiyai, oruvar kiccuk kāṭṭiya viṉaiyai iv viḷaiyāṭṭuk kuṟikkumpōlum!
2. aṭṭalaṅkāy puṭṭalaṅkāy
cevilittāy allatu mūtāy (pāṭṭi) pala kuḻantaikaḷai varicaiyākak kālnīṭṭi uṭkāravaittu, avarkaḷ kālkaḷai iṭa valamākavum vala iṭamākavum taṭavikkoṇṭu,
aṭṭalaṅkāy puṭṭalaṅkāy aṭukkaṭukkāy mātuḷaṅkāy
 paccarici kuttip paraṇmēlē vaittirukku
 tēṅkāy uṭaittut tiṇṇaimēlē vaittirukku
 māṅkāy uṭaittu maṭimēlē vaittirukku
 uppuk kaṇṭañ cuṭṭu uṟimēlē vaittirukku
 entap pūṉai tiṉṟatu - intap pūṉai tiṉṟatu
eṉṟu pāṭi, intap pūṉai eṉṟu collumpōtu oru kuḻantai yaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvāḷ.
pūṉai uṟiyiluḷḷa uppukkaṇṭattait tiṉṟuviṭṭataik kuṟippatāka uḷḷatu iv viḷaiyāṭṭu.
3
periyōr pakkam
iḷaiñarukku mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaiñarum mutiyōrum āṭum āṭṭut tokuti periyōr pakkamām.
1. āṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
tāyam
itu palavakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu patiṉaintu nāyum puliyum eṉpatām. itu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē āṭappaṭṭateṉpatu,
valeṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē	(tol. 373)
 nāyum palakaiyum varūuṅ kālai
 āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
 ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akaram nilaiyum	(. 374)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāl (cūttiraṅkaḷāl) aṟiyalām.
(2) iravāṭṭu
kaḻiyal
oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokaiyiṉar, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil neruṅki niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoru kaiyilum ovvoru kuṟuṅkaḻi ēnti avaṟṟaip piṟar kaḻikaḷōṭu tākkiyaṭittu, piṉṉip piṉṉiyum cuṟṟic cuṟṟiyum varum āṭṭu, kaḻiyal eṉappaṭum.
itu pakalilum āṭaṟkuriyatāyiṉum, potuvāka iravilēyē āṭappeṟum. itaṟkum kummikkup pōl oru taṉivakaip pāṭṭuṇṭu. atu,
 taṉṉaṉa taṉṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa - taṉa
taṉṉaṉa tāṉaṉa taṉṉāṉa
eṉṉum vaṇṇam paṟṟiyatākum.
(3) irupoḻutāṭṭu
mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
iruvarum avarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum, periya elumiccaṅkā yaḷavāṉa iruppukkuṇṭu āḷukkoṉṟu vaittukkoṇṭu, pappattuk kacam iṭaippaṭṭa mukkuḻikaḷil eṟintāṭuvatu, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
itu perumpāṉmai pakalilum ciṟupāṉmai nilavirali lum āṭappeṟum. tiruccirāppaḷḷi vaṭṭārattār itaic ciṟappāka āṭuvar.
peṇpāṟ pakuti
(1) pakalāṭṭu
1. paṇṇāṅkuḻi
itu iḷaiñar pakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
2. tāyam
paramapatam, ciluvaittāyam eṉa vaḻaṅkum kuṟukkuk kaṭṭat tāyam ākiya iraṇṭum, perumpāṉmaiyāka vaḻaṅkum peṇṭir tāya viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷām.
(2) irupoḻutāṭṭu
kummi
itu iḷaiñar pakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
talaiyāya kulattup peṇṭir potuvāka itai āṭuvatillai.
piṟkuṟippu : 54ām pakkattil varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷa pārikkōṭu (allatu pārikōṭu) eṉṉum viḷaiyāṭṭu, pāriyiṉ paṟampumalaiyai mūvēntarum muṟṟukaiyiṭṭiruntataik kuṟikkalām.
piṉṉiṇaippu
I. vaḻakkaṟṟa viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ
1. aṟiyappaṭṭavai
(1) āṇpāṟ pakuti
vaṭṭu
itu pakalil āṭappaṭum oruvakaic cūtāṭṭākum.
(2) peṇpāṟ pakuti
1. pala pantu
orutti 5 pantu koṇṭāṭiyatu cintāmaṇiyuḷḷum, irumakaḷir 7 pantum 12 pantum koṇṭāṭiyatu peruṅkataiyuḷ ḷum, kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai pakalāṭṭu.
2. ammāṉai
mūvar makaḷir muṟaiyē kūṟṟum viṉāvum viṭaiyumāka mukkūṟuṭaiyatum ammāṉai eṉṟiṟuvatumāṉa oruvakaik koccakak kalippāvaip pāṭikkoṇṭu, taṉittaṉi pala pantukaḷaip pōṭṭup piṭittu āṭum āṭṭu ammāṉaiyām. ituvum pakalāṭṭē.
uḻuvaiyuri yaraikkacaitta ulakamelā muṭaiyaperu
muḻumutalē karuvainakar mukantiruntār ammāṉai
muḻumutalē karuvainakar mukantiruntā rāmāyiṉ
eḷiyavarpōṟ kaḷavāṇṭa temmuṟaiyē ammāṉai
itaṉālaṉ ṟēmaṟaivāy irukkiṉṟār ammāṉai
eṉpatu ōr ammāṉaic ceyyuḷām.
3. kuravai
 eḻuvar allatu paṉṉiruvar makaḷir, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivantu pāṭiyāṭum kūttu, kuravaiyām. itu iru poḻutāṭ ṭākat terikiṉṟatu.
2. aṟiyappaṭātavai
cāḻal, teḷḷēṇam mutaliyaṉa.
pantu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭai mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu viḷaiyāṭum ōr āṭṭu, accuppūṭṭi viḷaiyāṭutal eṉṉum peyarāl, rāṭṭilar (Rottler) akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷatu.
II. paḷḷikkūṭa viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ
kōḻikkuñcu - 1
pala piḷḷaikaḷ, oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka oruvar iṭuppai iṉṉoruvar cērntu kaṭṭikkoṇṭu allatu paṟṟik koṇṭu, varicaiyāka niṟpar. talaimaiyāṉa piḷḷai mutalil niṟkum. ap piḷḷaikku etirē maṟṟoru piḷḷai niṟkum.
talaimaiyāṉa piḷḷai kōḻiyaiyum, piṉṉāl niṟkum piḷḷaikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷaiyum, etirē niṟkum piḷḷai kaḻukaiyum, nikarppar. kaḻuku kaṭaicik kuñcaip piṭikka varum. varicai vaḷaintu kuñcukaḷ tappum.
kaḻuku kōḻiyai nōkki eṉakkoru kuñcu tā; iṉṟēl paṟantu vantu piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭuvēṉ eṉṉum. kōḻi maṟuttu, kaḻuku kuñcaip piṭikkātapaṭi maṟikkum. kaḻuku cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vantu kaṭaicik kuñcaip piṭittukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉamē, piṉpu ēṉaik kuñcukaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟāyp piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭum.
kōḻi mika mika varuntum.
kōḻikkuñcu - 2
eṭṭup piḷḷaikaḷ cērntukoḷvar. avaruḷ oru piḷḷai kō ḻiyaiyum, aintu piḷḷaikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷaiyum, maṟṟoru piḷḷai kaḻukaiyum, maṟṟumoru piḷḷai nariyaiyum nikarppar.
kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷaik kūṇṭiṟkuḷ irukkac colliviṭṭu, iraitēṭa veḷiyē ceṉṟuviṭum. kuñcukaḷ tāy pēccait taṭṭik kūṇṭiṟku veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku oru kuñcai eṭuttukkoṇṭupōkum. ēṉaik kuñcukaḷellām kūṇṭiṟkuḷ ōṭiviṭum. piṉpu caṟṟunēram poṟuttu mīṇṭum veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku mīṇṭum oru kuñcai eṭuttukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉam nālu kuñcu pōṉapiṉ tāy tirumpivarum. eñciyuḷḷa kuñcu naṭantataic collum.
kōḻi kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, eṉ kuñcukaḷaiyellāṅ koṭuttuviṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup patilāka nāṉ uṉakku nāḷaikkuk kaṟitaruvēṉ eṉṟu vākkaḷittu, kuñcukaḷai mīṭṭukkoṇṭu pōkum.
maṟunāḷ, kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟi eṅṅaṉam koṭuppateṉṟu kōḻi kavaṉṟukoṇṭirukkumpōtu, nari ataṉiṭam vantu, nī kavalaippaṭātē. kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟikoṭukka vēṇṭiyatillai. ataṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟku uṉakkoru valakkarām (tantiram) collikkoṭuppēṉ, eṉṟu collum. kōḻi atai nampikkoṇṭu cummā iruntuviṭum. piṉpu nari kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, kōḻi uṉakku vākkaḷittapaṭi kaṟi koṭukkātām. nī pōy ataṉ kuñcukaḷaip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāltāṉ uṉakkuk kaṟi kiṭaikkum eṉṟu mūṭṭiviṭum. kaḻuku uṭaṉē pōy, ellāk kuñcukaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟākap piṭittukkeṇṭu vantuviṭum. narikku oru kuñcu paṅku kiṭaikkum.
kōḻi nariyai nampik keṭṭuppōṉatai niṉaittu mikat tuyaruṟum.
kuṟippu : mēṟkūṟiya viḷaiyāṭṭiraṇṭum pētaipparuvap peṇkaḷukkuriyavai. aiyāṭṭaip paruvattārāyiṉ āṇ piḷḷaikaḷum cērntukoḷḷalām.
III. paṇṭai viḷaiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷ
(1) puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭu
paṇṭaik kālattil, āṟṟarukē yirunta nakaramānta rellārum, āṇṭutōṟum āṟṟiṟ putuveḷḷam vantavuṭaṉ oruṅkē ceṉṟu, oru pakaliṟ perumpakuti av veḷḷattil tiḷaittāṭi iṉpuṟṟa viḷaiyāṭṭu viḻā, puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭu allatu puṉalāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. atu nīrviḻā, nīrāṭṭu, nīrāṭṭaṇi mutaliya peyar koṇṭum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
nīrāṭuvārellām, āṟṟiliṭuvataṟkup poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum ceyta mīṉ mutaliya kāṇikkaik karuvikaḷaiyum, puṇai teppam parical mutaliya mitavaik karuvikaḷaiyum, kātalarmītu vāca neyyaiyum vaṇṇa nīraiyum teḷittaṟkut turutti kompu civiṟi mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷaiyum, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vēṇṭum ūṇ uṭai akil mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, tattamakku iyaṉṟavāṟu, yāṉai kutirai tēr mutaliyavaṟṟil ūrntum kālnaṭaiyāy naṭantum, koṇṭu celvar. kuṭivāriyāka āṅkāṅku amaikkappeṟṟa kuṟṟilkaḷum putukkaṭaikaḷum, cērntu, oru viḻavūr pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum.
nīntavallār caṟṟu āḻattilum allātār karaiyai
yaṭuttum nīrāṭuvatum, pūcuñ cuṇṇam cāntu kuḻampu mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvupaṟṟip peṇṭir ikalāṭuvatum, tam kaṇavaṉmār piṟa peṇṭiroṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭiṉāreṉṟu maṉaikkiḻat tiyar ūṭuvatum, pūcucāntam puṉaintamālai uḻakkum nīrāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kalaṅkal veḷḷam putumaṇam peṟuvatum, puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷām.
nīrāṭiyavar mālaikkālattil makiḻntum ayarntum maṉai tirumpuvar.
nīrāṭṭu viḻā uñcai nakarattil 21 nāḷ nikaḻnta teṉap peruṅkatai kūṟum.
ik kālattuk koṇṭāṭappeṟum āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukku, paṇṭaip puṉalāṭṭai oru puṭaiyokkum.
(2) poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭu
nakaravāṇar, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil, ūrukkuc caṟṟut tolaiviluḷḷa oru cōlaikkuc ceṉṟu, taṉittaṉiyākavum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākavum oru pakal camaittuṇṭu makiḻnta viḻā, poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ikkālattil uṟaviṉarum naṇparumāka oru cilar ceṉṟuṇṇum kāṭṭuṇāp pōṉṟatākum.
cōlaiyai aṭaintapiṉ, aṭicil toḻilil īṭu
paṭṭavaroḻinta ēṉaiyarellām vevvēṟu viṉaipaṟṟi vevvēṟiṭañceṉṟu viṭuvar. āṭavaruḷ periyōr vēṭṭaiyāṭavum, ciṟiyōr maramēṟutal kāykaṉi paṟittuṇṭal viḷaiyāṭutal mutaliya viṉai nikaḻttavum, pirintuviṭuvar. peṇṭiruḷ mūttōr aṭicil toḻilil amara, iḷaiyōr, malar koytu mālai toṭukkavum, pāvai puṉaintu pārāṭṭi makiḻavum, ūñcalamaittu untiyāṭavum, ciṟṟiliḻaittuc ciṟucōṟu camaikkavum, āṅkāṅku akaṉṟuviṭuvar.
cilar kaṭṭamutu koṇṭucelvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṉṉār aṉaivarum iṉpa viḷaiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭuvar.
naṇpakal uṇavuṇṭapiṉ, cila nāḻikai nēram iḷaippāṟi mālaik kālam vīṭu tirumpuvatu iyalpākum.
putitāy maṇanta kātalar, tāmē ceṉṟu āṭum poḻilāṭṭum, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu potu nakara māntar koṇṭāṭum poḻilāṭṭum, vevvēṟām.
* * * * *
